"So, Little Yang, you mean to say that not only is the hidden enemy not dead, but he might also be right among us?"

Lu Yang nodded and explained, "Fairy, do you remember the incident in Buddha Country where we encountered a change in the timeline?"

"I remember, what about it?"

"Although we did not know the specific content of the timeline change made by the hidden enemy at the time, did you notice it was related to the event we encountered?"

"I think we touched upon a clue about the hidden enemy at that time, and that’s why he modified the timeline."

Lu Yang held up two fingers, "There are two possibilities: one, the hidden enemy may have set some taboo words in advance, and once these words are voiced, the timeline changes; we said one of those taboo words."

Saying this, Lu Yang’s expression turned serious and he spoke solemnly, "The other possibility is that he was hiding in the shadows, watching us."

"I don’t believe it was merely a coincidence that he was near us; it’s more likely he has always been lurking in the shadows, observing us!"

Thus, some things Lu Yang could not say in the outside world, but only in the Spiritual Space where both Yun Zhi and Fairy Eternity were present.

Fairy Eternity was startled by Little Yang’s speculation, feeling that Little Yang was even smarter than herself.

Yun Zhi roughly understood Lu Yang’s thoughts, only one last question remained, "Then why did you still agree to open the World Seal?"

Lu Yang gave a wry smile and spread his hands, "Because I have no reason not to agree to open the World Seal. The only reason not to agree is to think that the hidden enemy hasn’t died yet, and I’m waiting for the hidden enemy to think his feigned death was successful and expose a flaw himself."

"If I refused to open the World Seal, the hidden enemy would only hide deeper."

"To buy time, I said we couldn’t rashly remove the seal and that many things needed to be prepared."

"Eldest Senior Sister, what do you think?" Lu Yang looked at Yun Zhi with hopeful eyes, wishing she could propose a reason externally to prevent the opening of the Box World’s seal.

Yun Zhi hadn’t expected her Junior Brother to have thought so thoroughly.

"I also think the hidden enemy is faking death, but I believe it’s best to unlock the seal."

Chapter 1264: 1263: Fears of Innumerable Dangers Outside

"Ah? Why?" Lu Yang wondered.

But what puzzled him even more was that his question seemed to have been asked by someone just before.

"How do you assess the Battle of Zhongshan County?"

"The first in both ancient and modern times."

From ancient times to the present, in terms of scale and intensity, the Battle of Zhongshan County was undoubtedly ranked first.

"If there had been no barrier made by the Power of National Fate, what do you think the consequences of the Battle of Zhongshan County would have been?"

Lu Yang was stunned, realizing that he had never considered this before, and then he shivered.

Without a doubt, it would have been a war that brought untold suffering to all living beings!

The battle between Fairy Eternity and Ashy Bean alone was a clue; they fought from the Demon Territory to the Land of No Spirit and then looped back, saying they ascended to the heavens and descended to the earth was not an exaggeration.

With a larger scale for the Battle of Zhongshan County, if there had been no barrier to block it, wherever the battle unfolded, there would have been death and injury.

"Yao Saint is just a puppet created by the mastermind in the shadows, which shows that the true strength of the mastermind is even more formidable."

"The Power of National Fate accumulated from the Battle of Zhongshan County has been depleted by a large margin, and if I were to confront the real mastermind in the shadows, the Cultivation World would surely be destroyed."

"This world cannot withstand battles of such a level; our battleground can only be outside the Box World."

...

Within Captive Peak, the task of printing newspapers came down, and the content of the newspapers greatly shocked them... the Great Qian Force and Great Yu Force had temporarily ceased hostilities.

"Are they going to lift the World Seal?"

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord looked envious; everything he had done was to lift the Seal and see the outside world.

But now, ironically, the Seal was about to be lifted as he found himself imprisoned.

"What luck," Mid-heaven Emperor Lord did not know whether to cry or laugh.

"Look on the bright side; when Yao Saint broke into the Fairy Palace, he informed us that the clue to the Time Dao Fruit was at the Seek Dao Sect and mentioned that his goal regarding lifting the Seal was the same as ours," The Controller consoled him.

"At least we’re luckier than that Yao Saint; we can at least witness the process of the Seal being lifted, while he won’t even catch a glimpse."

"That is true," nodded Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

...

As the new edition of Cultivation Weekly was published, the entire Cultivation World was abuzz with this news.

"The Four Ancient Immortals have all appeared?! Are they going to lift the World Seal?!"

The Four Ancient Immortals were synonymous with age and mystery; for three hundred thousand years since the end of the Ancient Times, countless people searched for traces of the Immortals in vain.

Only the Yingtian Immortal had appeared after Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou’s Pass Tribulation.

Unexpectedly, the other three immortals showed up all at once!

"But what does it mean to lift the Box World Seal? Where is the Box World?" Ordinary people and the vast majority of cultivators spend their whole lives never leaving their counties or states, unaware of what the true appearance of the world is.

Amid the confusion, the text on the newspaper changed, forming a perspective cube pattern, and marked on the six faces of the cube where Great Xia was, where the Demon Territory was, where the East Sea was...

People felt as if they had emerged from the clouds to see the light of day, finally understanding the true form of the world.

Looking at the paper, did the world map expand?

"I thought the world was already vast, but there’s more world beyond our world."

"But this seems to have nothing to do with us, right?"

So what if the world map got bigger? What did it have to do with them?

What shocked them the most was still the appearance of the Four Ancient Immortals.

Those cultivators who knew the true form of the world, especially those who could cross the void with their physical bodies and shuttle through the cosmos, appeared extremely excited and agitated.

Many, without hesitation, spent a fortune to book entire taverns and brothels.

Some patrons of the brothels, upon hearing this news, came straight out of their rooms and excitedly chatted with their friends next door.

The family elders pondered in silence, nurturing a desire to venture into the outside world.

"We never thought, we would actually be fortunate enough to witness the day when the Box World opens!"

"It’s said that with the advent of the Great Era, the ancient ways would be restored, this must surely be a part of the coming of the Great Era!"

"What does the outside world actually look like?"

"It’s hard to say, the outside probably teems with danger."

"Why do you say that?"

"Think about it, our world is only so big, yet so many cultivators, semi-immortals, and immortals have emerged from it."

"According to Ancient Texts, the outside world is vast and boundless, there must be even more cultivators there, maybe meeting someone with a Cultivation Level like ours, a Body Integration Realm, could happen all the time!"

"In my opinion, when the day comes to lift the World Seal, we ought to go to the outside world together, to have each other’s support."

"That makes sense."

"The Dynasty will certainly send out an expedition team to investigate the outside world, what a pity that we are not officials of the court; otherwise, we could be much safer."

"Let’s pray to the Four Ancient Immortals that we don’t encounter cultivators much stronger than us once we go out."

The Land Money Trade Association saw the opportunity and began to offer personal accident insurance for those exploring the outside world.

...

Emperor Xia, Ming Yu Abbot, Zhu Tian, the Old Dragon Emperor, and the retired Snow Emperor, representing the major forces, engaged in detailed discussions about unraveling the World Seal.

Their representatives Jiang Pingan, Time Immortal, Jiang Lianyi, Ao Ling, and Immortal Jiuchong were hands-off managers and had only a vague understanding of their own forces’ situations; detailed discussions still required these real managers to step forward.

The appearance of the Snow Emperor surprised the other four, who hadn’t heard of a Snow Emperor in the Extreme Northern Lands.

"Snow Emperor, are you the ruler of the Extreme Northern Lands?"

The Snow Emperor sighed. He had barely enjoyed a few days of retirement before being called by Immortal Jiuchong to the meeting: "Not entirely, people in our area think I’m a legend, not a real person."

"Then..."

"But don’t worry, I’m the strongest in the Extreme Northern Lands, once our meeting is over, I can definitely implement the results there. If any of those brats don’t believe me, I don’t mind showing them what it means when the Snow Emperor manifests."

In his youth, the Snow Emperor was an undefeated figure in the Extreme Northern Lands, but after becoming a Semi-Immortal, he gradually became more reserved.

The others exchanged glances, but with the Snow Emperor putting it that way, it seemed there’s no need to worry about the cultivators from the Extreme Northern Lands being disobedient.

Emperor Xia was the first to speak: "When we unveil the Land of No Spirit, countless cultivators will surely venture out; this can’t be allowed to proceed unchecked, it wouldn’t be good for management. I believe we should set up a large Array Method, which could verify the identities of cultivators and check the items they carry, ensuring that anyone entering or leaving the world must pass through this Array Method."

However, Emperor Xia’s suggestion made everyone present somewhat uncomfortable, especially the Old Dragon Emperor and the Snow Emperor.

"Why is no one saying anything?"

"Er, we... in the territories we govern, we have never done identity registration for cultivators."

Verifying the identities of cultivators presumes knowing who the cultivators are, but the East Sea and Extreme Northern Lands obviously didn’t have the means to do so.

The Demon Territory was slightly better off, but Zhu Tian didn’t have a complete census of every member of the Demon Race.

"So, what we need to do first is to compile the identities of all cultivators and create identity certificates?" Emperor Xia sighed, realizing the preparatory work was more extensive than he had thought.

"Actually, there’s a method to save time," the Snow Emperor had a good idea.

"What’s the method?"

"We ask Time Immortal to stop time, and when we go door to door to register identities, we let the person being registered move during the Time Stop, and once registered, we make them stop moving again."

"That way, not only can we register the identities of all the cultivators in the world, but even if we were to register all the cultivators and ordinary people in the world, it would take less than a second."

Chapter 1265: 1264: The Meaning of Reincarnation

Outside, the world was abuzz with activity, all in motion for the soon-to-be-released World Seal.

Inside Lu Yang’s abode, all was quiet. Lu Yang sat on a grass mat, pondering what kind of Dao Fruit could help Eldest Senior Sister break free from her state of Harmonization.

"Fairy, do you have any ideas?" Lu Yang noticed that Immortal Fairy wasn’t as anxious as he was, still sporting her usual cheerful demeanor, seemingly already with a plan in mind.

"Of course, there is a way."

Lu Yang’s eyes brightened, the Fairy was indeed reliable at crucial moments. "What way?"

"Wait until I ascend to the realm of ’Omniscience,’ then I won’t be ignorant of any answers, and solving Miss Yun’s problem will be a piece of cake."

Lu Yang: "..."

Upon reflection, he realized the Fairy’s moments of reliability were rare; it was still better to rely on himself.

Should Lu Yang wish, he could at any time solidify the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit and become the most powerful Semi-Immortal.

But clearly, the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit was not his path.

As for other existing Dao Fruit Outlines, such as the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit Prototype, the Spatial Dao Fruit Prototype... if he put in effort, he could also solidify them, but they wouldn’t solve Eldest Senior Sister’s issue either.

"Observing different Dao Fruit Prototypes might provide new insights," Lu Yang mused, believing that more thought and observation would lead to new ideas.

He was very familiar with the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit, no need for observation there. Should he next visit Senior Ao Ling?

Suddenly, Lu Yang paused, realizing there was another kind of Dao Fruit Prototype close at hand.

He took out the Green Peak Sword, pulled out the small sapling and the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype from the Small World.

After Qilin Immortal acquired the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, Immortal Fairy found it interesting and took it for herself. She hadn’t yet returned it to the Qilin Immortal when Yao Saint took action.

Naturally, the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype fell into Lu Yang’s hands, and with so much happening since, even Lu Yang forgot he still possessed it.

Was this what resided within Junior Brother Li’s body?

At the thought, Lu Yang slowly closed his eyes, adjusted his breathing rhythm, cleared his Lingtai, and reached a state of self-forgetfulness.

It was as if the world turned blank, with only the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype on the tree remaining.

Lu Yang’s mind flickered with countless memories, endlessly receding—experiences from his Transcendance Tribulation Stage to those of the Void Refinement Stage, further back to Spirit Transformation Stage, Nascent Soul Stage, Golden Core Stage, Foundation Establishment Stage, Qi Cultivation Stage, before joining the Seek Dao Sect... and even before his transmigration.

Gradually, the blank world began sprouting lines, messy at first, unrecognizable. But as Lu Yang reminisced about his life before transmigration, the chaotic lines took shape, forming Lu Yang’s likeness.

The blank world shattered, and Lu Yang abruptly opened his eyes, gasping for air, as if he had held his breath through a long and exhausting run; now, at its end, fatigue was bursting forth in full.

"Little Yang, are you alright?" Immortal Fairy kept a close eye on Lu Yang, and she asked with concern.

Lu Yang waved a hand, signaling to Immortal Fairy not to worry; he was still too exhausted to speak.

It took a good while for Lu Yang to recover, after which he shared his findings: "Not only am I fine, but I also feel connected to this Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype. With just a thought, this Dao Fruit Prototype could become mine."

"That means you can master the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype now." Immortal Fairy was surprised, Little Yang mastered it quickly.

Lu Yang analyzed, realizing that such quick mastery couldn’t be solely attributed to intuition: "Perhaps it’s related to my experiences outside the Box World. Having died once outside it, I had essentially undergone a reincarnation, which, by coincidence, matched the intent of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype."

"Do you want to become a Semi-Immortal of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?"

Lu Yang shook his head. Possessing the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype merely meant reincarnation after death, not the effect he desired.

Yet he had a hunch, feeling that the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype was somehow linked to his becoming a Semi-Immortal.

In the blink of an eye, more than ten days passed. The doors to Lu Yang’s abode swung open; between two large trees at the entrance hung a hammock, where Yun Mengmeng was lost in slumber.

As soon as there was a stir at the door of the abode, Yun Mengmeng swayed the hammock, spinning in mid-air before landing in front of Lu Yang.

"Second-in-command, have you finished your seclusion?"

Lu Yang shook his head, "No, I came out for a break because I got tired along the way."

"Ah? You can take breaks in the middle of seclusion?" Yun Mengmeng never had her own closed-door cultivation, so she didn’t understand these things.

"Sister Mengmeng, would you accompany me for a walk around the sect?"

"Of course."

The two descended the mountain and Lu Yang found, to his puzzlement, that the sect was unusually deserted compared to normal days.

"Where are the people from the sect?"

Yun Mengmeng pointed towards the entrance of the Seek Dao Sect, "Today is the day we recruit new disciples. Everyone has gone there to maintain order."

"So it’s already the second day of the second month again," Lu Yang said, somewhat in a daze. He thought back to when he was very naive, riding on Old Meng’s carriage, even discussing how to cheat in front of Eldest Senior Sister. So many years had passed since then.

When they arrived at the entrance of the sect, the first test of root bone was still the same, with Elder Brother Dai still sitting at the front conducting the tests.

The only difference from the time Lu Yang participated was an astonishing surge in the number of young men and women applying to the Seek Dao Sect—more than double the previous amount.

"Look, is that Lu Yang?"

A young boy in line excitedly pointed at Lu Yang.

The reason so many people came to the Seek Dao Sect was precisely because of the reputations of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

These two geniuses shone brightly, young in age but with experiences far richer than those of many older cultivators who had passed their Tribulation.

"Where? Where?"

"Right there at the bamboo forest entrance, look."

"It really is him!"

"Who’s that standing next to Lu Yang, so beautiful. How come we never heard of her?"

"I don’t know."

The previously well-ordered line of young men and women became slightly restless; Lu Yang suddenly the center of attention, forcing Elder Brother Dai to step in to maintain order and not forgetting to glare back at Lu Yang in the process.

Although Lu Yang hadn’t done anything wrong, he still retreated guiltily back into the bamboo forest out of sight from the young men and women after being glared at by Dai Bufan.

"Senior Brother."

"Senior Brother Lu."

"Junior Sister Tao, Junior Brother Li, you are here to help organize the initiation test?" Lu Yang said with a smile, his hands clasped in greeting.

"Yes, Elder Brother Dai said there was a shortage of reliable help, so he called us over," Li Haoran said with a smile.

"Senior Brother Lu, we haven’t seen each other for a long time," Tao Yaoye said with a hint of complaint in her voice, especially seeing Yun Mengmeng so close to Lu Yang.

"I’ve been quite busy recently, really didn’t have time," Lu Yang said, feeling somewhat embarrassed.

While chatting idly, Lu Yang’s gaze occasionally drifted over to the young men and women to see how their testing was progressing.

Tao Yaoye noticed Lu Yang’s gaze and remarked, "It’s easy to be reminded of when we took the test ourselves, isn’t it?"

"Yes," Li Haoran agreed with the same nostalgia.

They had all been obscure figures once upon a time. In the blink of an eye, so many years had passed, and now they had become figures spoken of by everyone.

At these words, Lu Yang was stunned; he had an epiphany, realizing his understanding of reincarnation had been too narrow. With Li Haoran as an example, he thought that only the transmigration of souls counted as reincarnation.

What’s to say that the recurrence of similar events isn’t a kind of reincarnation?

Chapter 1266: 1265: Lu Yang’s Dao Fruit Outline

Lu Yang carefully recalled the events he had experienced, each time he left the Sect over some trifling matter to gain experience in the outside world, only for the situation to escalate to an uncontrollable extent, which was in fact, a cycle of reincarnation.

In truth, he had always been within the grasp of reincarnation without realizing it.

"Could I be considered the origin of reincarnation, in a way?" he mused softly to himself.

"It’s not only the experiences that repeat; cultivation is a form of reincarnation as well."

"From Qi Refinement’s nine layers to the early, mid, and late stages of Foundation Establishment, then to the early, mid, and late stages of Golden Core... Advancing to a new level means starting a new cycle of reincarnation within that level, ceaselessly cultivating."

"Birth, aging, sickness, death, viewing the next generation as an extension of one’s own spirit, then the next generation facing birth, aging, sickness, death, and viewing the following generation as an extension of their spirit..."

"Does that mean, in essence, everyone is caught in the cycle of reincarnation?"

Lu Yang murmured softly under his breath, a hint of brightness in his eyes, as he finally understood the nature of the Dao Fruit that could help his Eldest Senior Sister.

He still needed a bridge to reach the Dao Fruit he sought; this bridge was made of knowledge, but the knowledge he had now was not enough. He needed to examine other Dao Fruit embryos, or even completed Dao Fruits!

He was uncertain which Dao Fruit embryos or Dao Fruits could bring him the knowledge he needed, so he had to resort to the most straightforward approach, to keep trying.

"Thank you, Junior Sister Tao, and thank you, Junior Brother Li!" Lu Yang said, bowing with his hands clasped together in solemn thanks, leaving Tao Yaoye and Li Haoran somewhat bewildered, but they returned the gesture in kind.

Lu Yang left everyone behind and headed straight for Captive Peak.

Fairy Eternity, filled with curiosity, asked, "Little Yang, have you thought of what kind of Dao Fruit embryo you want to condense?"

"I have!"

"What kind of Dao Fruit embryo is it? Will it involve Omniscience or Transcendence?"

As Lu Yang ran, he spoke, his heart somewhat excited: "Omniscience or Transcendence, either is fine. The important thing is that it helps the Eldest Senior Sister solve the issue with Harmonization. But whether it will actually work, only trying it out will tell!"

Arriving at Captive Peak, the forces of Great Yu and Great Qian, who rarely avoided conflict, were distinctly divided into two separate parts. Duan Qingshui and Mo Xiaochou, the two prisoners with the lowest Cultivation Levels, were also split up, one on the side of Great Yu and the other on the side of Great Qian.

A group of Semi-Immortals in the Tribulation Crossing Stage surrounded the two of them, seemingly teaching them knowledge.

Without giving it much thought, Lu Yang went straight to Guan Shanhai, earning a provocative look from Great Yu’s forces toward Great Qian’s as he approached.

"Do you need something? Is it time to print more newspapers?"

"Uh, no, I wanted to ask about your insights on condensing a Dao Fruit."

Lu Yang was curious about what life on Captive Peak was like now, how Guan Shanhai could be so preoccupied with printing newspapers, as it wasn’t like this before.

The insights on condensing a Dao Fruit were extremely precious, but it wasn’t something that couldn’t be shared. During his time in Captive Peak, Guan Shanhai had explained everything, including his insights on condensing a Dao Fruit.

However, back then, he had spoken to Yun Zhi; this time, it was with Lu Yang.

It was merely a matter of repeating the same information.

After seeking advice from Guan Shanhai, Lu Yang went over to the forces of Great Qian and successively asked The Controller and the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord for their experiences in condensing a Dao Fruit.

Nor did he overlook the Longevity Dao Fruit Prototype of Master of All Laws, the Void Realm Dao Fruit Prototype of Void Venerate, and the Dream Realm Dao Fruit Prototype of Nightmare Supreme.

After leaving Captive Peak, Lu Yang doubled back to Sky Gate Mountain and sought the counsel of Qinghe on the Longevity Dao Fruit Prototype, then asked the puppet to unlock the treasury.

He had thought that the puppet would need to report to the Eldest Senior Sister and get her approval first, but, to his surprise, the puppet opened the treasury directly.

The treasury at the end of Seek Dao Sect housed its most precious treasures, Wu Yao’s Swap Dao Fruit, Guan Shanhai’s Imprinting Dao Fruit, The Controller’s Enlightenment Dao Fruit, and Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s Devouring Dao Fruit.

Among them, the Devouring Dao Fruit was covered by a special Array Method, otherwise, it would devour everything, including the Dao Fruits nearby.

Lu Yang sat in front of these Dao Fruits, recalling the words of Guan Shanhai and others in his mind, seizing the moment to comprehend these Dao Fruits, to further understand the principles they had conveyed.

After an indeterminate time, Lu Yang left the treasury, shaking his head in disappointment. These pieces of knowledge were not what he was seeking.

"I need to continue learning."

Next, Lu Yang found Chasing Moon True Person on the back mountain of Sky Gate Mountain and sought teachings on the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, found Ancestor Guiyuan to inquire about the Guiyi Tao Fruit Prototype, and asked Celestial Master Guiyuan to take him to visit the other three founders who established the Three Immortal Sects.

Meng Junzi had taken Meng Jingzhou to show off in front of these people, and during his patrols in Quyi County, Meng Jingzhou had talked about these matters.

Having bid farewell to the founders of the Immortal Sects, Lu Yang arrived at the Dragon Palace of the East Sea, where he met Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, Jin Caiwei, and Vast Sea Ancestral Master and learned their understanding of the Dao Fruit Embryo one by one.

Lu Yang traveled across the entire Cultivation World to visit each Half-Immortal he knew and humbly learned from their experiences and insights.

Great Xia, East Sea, Demon Territory, Buddha Country... all bore the footprints of Lu Yang, who, like a mortal, measured the paths he had trodden with his steps, reminiscing on these experiences, while digesting the knowledge he had learned during his travels.

Some knowledge was easily understood by Lu Yang, resulting in a short stay; some were entirely new to him and difficult to comprehend, hence the prolonged stays.

And the place he stayed the longest was the Great Xia Imperial City.

There were two of the founders of Great Xia, Jiang Pingan and Meng Junzi, as well as the Four Ancient Immortals who had been invited to discuss the Dao – a total of six Immortals.

Along the way, Lu Yang saw that major forces were tallying the identities of Cultivators, observed the jointly enacted laws for exploration of the external world, and witnessed Great Cultivators rallying peers to form tight-knit teams ready for exploration.

He was like a passerby, witnessing the changes in the Cultivation World before the seals were lifted.

Mortals were indifferent to the unsealing, seemingly disassociated, but Lu Yang knew that once a planet with a technological civilization was found, bringing back those technologies and combining them with the knowledge of the Cultivation World would indeed concern them.

If it were not for the dark hand behind the scenes, this would be one of the reasons why Lu Yang supported the unsealing.

In the end, he returned to Seek Dao Sect, to the very beginning of everything.

Lu Yang made a low-profile return, not having told anyone.

All of this did not escape Yun Zhi’s perception, yet she said nothing, simply sipping on tea with the teaware from her Junior Brother, watching as her fully rewarded Junior Brother returned to his Abode for seclusion.

Lu Yang began an immeasurable period of seclusion in his Abode.

His journey could indeed be called fruitful, and at last, he had found the knowledge he most desired.

"Time Immortal’s Time Path, this is the ’bridge’ that links the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype and the Dao Fruit Prototype I want!"

The Time Path was the main body of the "bridge," and the other gains along the way were also parts of this "bridge."

He was like the raging sea, with a breath that rose and fell, surging and descending like rolling thunder, followed by the calm after the storm, his Realm once more advancing a layer.

"Middle Tribulation Stage now," he noted.

Unlike the excitement of previous realm breakthroughs, ascending to the Middle Tribulation Stage was simply going with the flow. What truly excited Lu Yang was the Dao Fruit Prototype he had condensed.

A Dao Fruit Prototype, previously unseen, emerged at Lu Yang’s chest, flickering with a pure white light before merging into his body.

"Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, achieved."

Chapter 1267: 1266: Tracing the Origin, Returning to the Starting Point

"Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype?" Fairy Eternity repeated quizzically.

"Tracing the root, returning to the starting point."

Although he had explained, Fairy Eternity still didn’t understand, "What’s the use of this?"

Lu Yang chuckled, "Just wait, I’ll find someone to practice on and then you’ll see what it does."

Lu Yang stretched leisurely, as he had lost track of time while comprehending the Dao Fruit Outline, not knowing how long his seclusion had lasted. But it surely wasn’t a short period, as his body had stiffened.

He walked out of his abode, the morning sunlight strikingly bright.

"Why are there so many herbs?"

Lu Yang was taken aback, looking out over fields of medicinal herbs spreading from the mountain peak to the foothills, plants of great age that even top-grade spirit stones might not procure, each one would cause a frenzy among Integration Period Cultivators if placed in the outside world.

Just how long had I been in seclusion for, and how did Sky Gate Mountain turn into a medicine garden?

Yun Mengmeng wore a straw hat, bent over with a jade vase in hand, catching dew droplets rolling off the leaf tips.

"Second-in-command, you’ve come out of seclusion?"

Yun Mengmeng, holding the jade vase, trotted across the fields towards Lu Yang, "Just in time to try some dew I’ve just collected."

"Dew?"

Was Sister Mengmeng collecting dew just now?

"I’ve just discovered it recently; the dew in the morning is very refreshing."

Lu Yang, puzzled, "Why not collect it directly with a magic skill?"

It seemed so cumbersome to catch dew drop by drop with a jade vase when a magic skill could gather several bottles in an instant.

"This is called ’living’."

Lu Yang nodded, pretending to understand, although he didn’t, he respected the notion.

He took a sip of the dew and, whether due to psychology or the true taste, remarked, "It is indeed refreshing."

"Right?"

"What’s going on with this medicine field? Has the medicine garden moved to our Sky Gate Mountain?"

"It’s just that it was quite a hassle to keep going to the medicine garden to pick herbs for cooking, so Little Lotus and I planted some here on the mountain. They are growing incredibly well; one-year herbs are growing as though they were ten years old."

"Little Lotus said it’s because I possess the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, and she has the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo; both can promote the growth of the herbs."

This reminded Lu Yang of how enticing Fairy’s Eternity Dao Fruit was to the Little Medicinal Kings.

"Right, Chief Housekeeper and Second-in-command, let’s go over there, there’s something good." Yun Mengmeng suddenly recalled something and, without further ado, pulled Lu Yang towards the middle of the mountain.

Arriving mid-mountain, they were greeted by a riot of purples and reds, flowers enveloping the entire Sky Gate Mountain, their scent intoxicating.

"What is this?"

"It’s to celebrate the Second-in-command’s emergence from seclusion, quite beautiful, don’t you think?"

"This was an idea Little Lotus and I had, from the bottom of the mountain to the top, there are twelve different flowering periods. Starting from January, the herbs at the foot of the mountain bloom, all the way to the herbs at the peak in December."

"This way, Second-in-command, whenever you come out of seclusion, you’ll always see flowers," Yun Mengmeng explained with an innocent smile.

Fairy Eternity floated out, giving a casual but complacent remark, "Pretty, isn’t it? Especially for Little Yang?"

Without a second thought, Yun Mengmeng replied, "It’s also for the Chief Housekeeper, right? You also had a hard time accompanying the Second-in-command during his seclusion."

"Humph, as long as you know that Immortal too had a tough time."

Yun Mengmeng always felt that the Chief Housekeeper’s reaction was a bit strange, but didn’t dwell on it, "Second-in-command, you’ve been in seclusion for so long, is it finally over?"

"Of course, do you see who I am?"

"You’ve condensed a Dao Fruit Outline, what’s it for?" Yun Mengmeng was very curious, her eyes sparkling.

Lu Yang hesitated for a moment, feeling embarrassed to test it on Yun Mengmeng, "Let’s find Old Meng and try it on him."

On the way, Lu Yang asked, "Isn’t Senior Qinghe around?"

"The Demon Territory’s Creation Ancient Land has a lot of medicinal herbs that have been lost to time," Qinghe thought there were not enough varieties, so she went there to look for herbs."

The Creation Ancient Land was precisely where Jiang Lianyi had once fallen into slumber; it housed many ancient medicinal herbs. Lu Yang had considered getting a few of those herbs, but they were too delicate and couldn’t adapt to the external environment.

It seemed that Qinghe had a way of making them adapt to the outside world.

Most members of the Immortal Lineage came to Meng Jingzhou’s abode, coincidentally meeting him just as he stretched and walked out from his retreat.

"Old Meng, just out of retreat, did you make a breakthrough?" No need to sense his cultivation with Spiritual Sense, from Old Meng’s show-off expression alone, Lu Yang could be certain that Old Meng definitely had broken through.

"So-so, just Middle Tribulation Stage," Meng Jingzhou said modestly on the surface but gleaned information from Old Lu’s show-off expression.

"Did you also make a breakthrough?"

"Only a Semi-Immortal, nothing to make a fuss about."

"You actually succeeded?!" Meng Jingzhou was surprised; he had heard before the retreat that Lu Yang went around the world in search of clues to form the initial shape of Dao Fruits.

To his surprise, Lu Yang already had Semi-Immortal status with Mid-stage Tribulation Crossing Cultivation upon his return.

"Shall we spar somewhere?"

"Let’s do it!"

Both having made breakthroughs, they were eager to test their current abilities.

They chose a deserted area deep within the Seek Dao Sect. Yun Mengmeng was watching the battle from a tree.

"Let’s make it clear, we rely on our own strength; no using Imitation Fist for you, and I won’t borrow cultivation from our ancestors."

Meng Jingzhou recalled the last time he fought Old Lu who shamelessly used underhanded tactics—utilizing not only Imitation Fist to borrow cultivation but also the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruits. It would be a miracle if he could win against that.

"Okay."

In this deserted area surrounded by mountains stood a vast lake, and the two stood on the surface of the water, appearing as if they were nothing; the lake remained calm as a mirror.

Meng Jingzhou’s muscles bulged, veins popped, and his body was tense like a cheetah waiting for its prey to make a move.

Lu Yang drew out his Green Peak Sword, infusing the outline of the Trace Origin Dao Fruit into the blade.

Seeing Lu Yang move, Meng Jingzhou, who had been preparing for a long time, suddenly burst into action with a loud boom, raising a colossal wave in the lake. His speed was much faster than the waves—it looked as if the waves were chasing his footsteps from afar!

Lu Yang simply performed what seemed to be an ordinary set of Sword Skills, striking Meng Jingzhou’s tough, wall-like flesh. Meng Jingzhou raised his hand to block!

Clang!

The Green Peak Sword struck Meng Jingzhou, leaving behind only a shallow sword mark, and a drop of Pure Yang True Blood!

"Old Lu, didn’t you eat? Your strength is lacking!" Meng Jingzhou laughed heartily—such an injury he could heal in an instant with a single thought!

A subtle, inscrutable smile appeared on Lu Yang’s lips: "Is that really so?"

Meng Jingzhou was taken aback, "What do you mean?"

Before he could finish his sentence, his expression changed dramatically, as the realm he’d just broken into vanished into nothingness!

In just the blink of an eye, he had fallen from Middle Tribulation Stage to Early Tribulation Stage!

"What is this about?!"

Meng Jingzhou was certain he hadn’t been caught in an Illusion Technique; it was a true and direct fall in his realm!

Lu Yang’s face still bore that same smile.

Meng Jingzhou finally understood what Lu Yang was smiling about.

"The Initial Form of Dao Fruit that I condensed, the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, traces the root and returns to the origin. All things are in the cycle of Reincarnation, and thus is life, and so is cultivation."

"A more straightforward explanation is that it makes you return to the time just when you broke through to the Transcendence Tribulation Stage."

Meng Jingzhou’s pupils dilated – to return to the starting point, to lower one’s cultivation, was an ability against the natural order!

Fairy Eternity was suddenly enlightened; finally, she understood the purpose of Little Yang’s Dao Fruit Embryo and was very pleased.

"Isn’t this just suppressing your enemy’s realm lower than your own and then defeating them? Little Yang, you’ve learned the essence of the Invincible Elixir!"

Lu Yang: "..."

It’s not Fairy; how did my profound and inscrutable powers become so vulgar in your mouth?

Chapter 1268: 1267: Strange Trace Origin Dao Fruit Outline

"Fairy, the prototype of my Trace Origin Dao Fruit represents the vastness of life, having been through two lifetimes and comprehended reincarnation. Drawing from the Time Dao Fruit, traversing the Cultivation World to seek masters and make friends, visiting countless Semi-Immortals and Immortals to discuss life, learning their Dao Fruit philosophies, it’s a culmination of all these experiences. It’s not just about reducing cultivation levels... alright, it is about overcoming opponents by reducing their cultivation levels."

After half a day of explanation, Lu Yang realized it was indeed what Fairy Eternity had said.

Damn it, could it be that I was able to condense the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype because it related to the fusion with the Invincible Primordial Spirit?

According to Lu Yang’s expectation, as long as he could become an Immortal, his Trace Origin Dao Fruit would be able to weaken an Immortal into a Semi-Immortal, and Eldest Senior Sister’s Harmonization could save her.

Sitting not far away on a tree, watching the battle, Yun Mengmeng’s eyes were filled with admiration. The Second-in-command is so powerful, lowering the opponent’s cultivation level is a surefire strategy, why didn’t she think of that?

"You can’t win this fight." Understanding the principle of the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, Meng Jingzhou immediately surrendered.

If both of them were at the Mid-stage Tribulation Crossing Cultivation, they could have had a match, but he was just at the Early Tribulation Stage, and Old Lu was at the Middle Tribulation Stage. There’s no point in fighting.

No matter how capable he was, he couldn’t fight above his level against Old Lu.

Surrendering now, he could avoid physical pain.

"Don’t give up just like that, we haven’t even started fighting, where’s your spirit?" Lu Yang genuinely wanted to fight Meng Jingzhou, the previous fight didn’t even count as a warm-up.

"You’re misunderstanding, brother. What’s our relationship? Could I possibly fight above my level against you?"

Lu Yang snapped his fingers, dispelling the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, and Meng Jingzhou returned to the Middle Tribulation Stage.

"How about this, I won’t use the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, and we’ll fight for real, how’s that?"

"That’s better."

The two stood on the lake surface, rallied their spirits, and adopted their stances once more.

In an instant, they both vanished from their spots, reappearing at the center of the lake, engaging in the most primitive combat without any techniques or moves.

Boom—

The lake surface exploded, water splashed up, and rained down like a downpour.

The two silhouettes flitted in and out of the curtain of rain created by the lake water, appearing like they had thousands of clones manifesting at various points within the rain curtain. The sounds of battle were like drumbeats, booming incessantly, setting hearts pounding fiercely.

Actually, it was due to their speed being so fast that ordinary people could only see afterimages, unable to discern where their true forms were amidst the myriad afterimages.

"Old Lu, not bad, neither your realm nor your physical body has fallen behind."

"You have to train your physical body; otherwise, Eldest Senior Sister would toss you straight into the furnace to burn."

"If you’re so afraid of burning, how will you withstand this move!"

Pure Yang True Flame and Nirvana True Flame suddenly appeared beside Meng Jingzhou. The appearance of these two extremely powerful flames caused the lake to boil, the water to evaporate, and mist to fill the air, making it even harder to see their figures.

"You’re trying to ambush me!" Lu Yang was furious. A good close-combat fight, yet you use magic skills, and the key point is I wasn’t the one ambushing first.

"That’s called preemptive action!" Meng Jingzhou didn’t believe for one second that Old Lu hadn’t thought about ambushing.

The Pure Yang True Flame and Nirvana True Flame displayed by Mid-stage Tribulation Crossing Cultivation were terrifyingly powerful. Both True Flames required the nurturing of Pure Yang Aura, which was precisely what Meng Jingzhou had in abundance!

"True Fire, eh? I can do that too!"

The Pure Yang True Flame shone like the great sun, illuminating all things, deterring the petty and weak, while the Nirvana True Fire carried the sound of a phoenix’s cry, transforming into a blazing fire phoenix!

The two True Flames had already been refined to the utmost extreme by Meng Jingzhou!

Lu Yang’s palm steamed, and two kinds of True Fire appeared.

"Three Flavored True Fire, Golden Bird True Flame!"

Just as Meng Jingzhou was about to mock Old Lu for overestimating himself, he saw the Three Flavored True Fire and Golden Bird True Flame suddenly expand by tens of millions of times, like two flaming creatures. Especially the Golden Bird True Flame, it was more nimble than his Nirvana True Flame, and hotter than the Great Sun True Flame!

When had Old Lu refined these two True Fires to such an ultimate degree behind his back?!

The Three Flavored True Fire and Golden Bird True Flame compressed the void and scorched the sky, like the monarch of fire!

"Old Meng, move aside, my True Fire is tricky, it’s stimulated by the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype!" Lu Yang did not know what had happened on the other side and urgently transmitted his voice through his Spiritual Sense.

"It’s too late!" The True Fire was closing in, and Meng Jingzhou had no way out; he could only choose to clash directly!

At the moment the four True Fires collided, Meng Jingzhou’s face changed dramatically. Only after coming into contact with Old Lu’s True Fire did he realize how terrifying these two kinds of flame were!

Boom——

The lake water evaporated, Meng Jingzhou was blown to the shore, his clothes charred.

"Old Meng, are you alright?"

The sudden turn of events gave Lu Yang quite a scare. He hurried back to check the situation, and Yun Mengmeng also rushed over upon seeing it.

"Cough cough——" Meng Jingzhou was covered in soot and looked quite disheveled, "Still alive, I guess."

Only because he was tough, plus his Yang Qi was very compatible with the Yang Qi of the Golden Bird True Flame, counteracting the force. If it had been another Transcendance Tribulation Stage cultivator, they would have been seriously injured and already drinking Elixir from a gourd.

"When did your True Fire become so powerful, how come I’ve never seen you use it before?" Meng Jingzhou said with a resentful expression.

Lu Yang was just as innocent: "I had no idea my True Fire could be so fierce!"

"I originally intended to use the Three Flavored True Fire and Golden Bird True Flame as a decoy to divert attention and take the opportunity to launch a surprise attack on you."

"But as soon as I deployed these two True Fires, the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype was also activated, and you saw the effect: the power of the True Fire became so strong."

Meng Jingzhou said angrily, "We agreed not to use Dao Fruit Prototypes before the battle!"

Lu Yang quickly explained, "I didn’t want to use it; the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype acted on its own, like... like..."

Lu Yang thought for a long time, finally coming up with the precise description, "It’s like there are myriad connections between the two True Fires and the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype. If these two True Fires are used, the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype must be activated simultaneously."

"Fairy, do you know what’s going on?"

Immortal Fairy Eternity was just as puzzled, and the question was directed at her: "I’ve never seen this kind of circumstance. The power of these two True Fires is already comparable to True Fire deployed by a Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype."

The reason why Semi-Immortals are far superior to those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage is precisely because of the existence of Dao Fruit Prototypes, which make the magic skills deployed through them much more powerful than normal.

For instance, a Peak Tribulation Stage cultivator deploying the Great Divine Skill "Five Thunders Technique" would definitely be inferior to to the Thunder Skill deployed by a Tribulation Dao Fruit Prototype.

That’s why just now, the Three Flavored True Fire and Golden Bird True Flame could overwhelmingly dominate and forcefully suppress the Pure Yang True Fire and the Nirvana True Flame.

"Why would the Second-in-command’s Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype make the two True Fires stronger?"

It’s easy to understand that the Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype would make True Fire stronger, given that True Fire is ’Yang’, but Yun Mengmeng couldn’t figure out: what’s the connection between Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype and the two True Fires?

"And another thing, why must I activate the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype simultaneously when I deploy the two True Fires?"

Lu Yang frowned, formulating a guess in his heart, "Unless these two True Fires themselves are the result of ’Trace Origin’?"

Chapter 1269: 1268: The Magic Skill I’ve Learned is the Correct One!

"The result of ’Trace Origin’ itself?"

Meng Jingzhou heard the implications in Lu Yang’s words, and his gaze became somewhat strange, "Do you mean that the ’Samadhi True Fire’ was originally called ’Three Flavored True Fire,’ and ’Gold Crow True Flame’ was actually ’Golden Bird True Flame’?"

Lu Yang remained calm and composed, as if on the brink of enlightenment, "That is pretty much what I mean."

"Don’t disbelieve me; I’m serious," Lu Yang felt his speculation made perfect sense.

"Fairy, who created the Samadhi True Fire and the Gold Crow True Flame?"

Fairy Eternity recalled all she had learned in her life and managed to give an answer, "Both of these True Fires were born in an extremely distant past. They are far older than our times and probably originated during the era when the Cultivation System was being created."

"It’s hard to say exactly who created the Samadhi True Fire, but the Gold Crow True Flame was most likely created by the Gold Crow lineage."

Lu Yang clapped his hands, "That fits perfectly."

"Even though the ancient ancestors lived in harsh conditions, battling against heaven and earth, since they had created True Fire magic skills, it meant they had the very basic ability to defend themselves, so hunting wild beasts wasn’t difficult."

"Once they had taken care of food and warmth, the next natural step was to figure out how to eat better."

"Seasonings like chili, cumin, and sugar must have been hard to find during Ancient Times, so the ancient ancestors created ’Three Flavored True Fire,’ a fire that could enhance flavors—capable of both cooking the meat and making it tastier, killing two birds with one stone!"

"And there’s another thing, the existence of writing had just begun in Ancient Times. Could our ancestors have come up with a sophisticated name like ’Samadhi’ for True Fire? Definitely not."

"They interpreted the words literally; the True Fire had three flavors, so they named it ’Three Flavored True Fire.’"

"Then for some reason, Three Flavored True Fire was lost, or only the name survived, and later generations changed it wildly, turning it into Samadhi True Fire."

"Look, does that sound logical?"

Clapping came from both sides simultaneously, Fairy Eternity and Yun Mengmeng applauding and vocalizing support for Lu Yang.

"Little Yang’s ideas are just what this Immortal wanted to express."

"The Second-in-command makes such reasonable points."

Meng Jingzhou, "..."

Don’t think that just because your Immortal Lineage has many people you are correct.

"Then how do you explain the Golden Bird True Flame?"

Before Lu Yang could answer, Yun Mengmeng raised her hand and answered first, "I know this one, definitely the ancient Gold Crow lineage had two legs, and later they mutated to have three."

The biggest difference between Golden Bird True Flame and Gold Crow True Flame is the loss of one leg.

Lu Yang echoed, "See, that’s the answer."

The three from the Immortal Lineage agreed, leaving Meng Jingzhou with some doubts, "... Maybe you should test another magic skill?"

Old Lu didn’t just learn the two True Fire skills crookedly.

"Let me try Earth Shrinking and Shrink Inch."

Lu Yang stood up without hesitation and, as usual, employed Earth Shrinking.

With a whoosh, he burrowed underground. Meng Jingzhou wanted to observe Old Lu’s underground movements using his Spiritual Sense, but Lu Yang moved through the soil so quickly it was surprising, and he soon exceeded the range of Meng Jingzhou’s Spiritual Sense.

When Lu Yang emerged from the ground, he was holding a handful of black soil.

Meng Jingzhou’s pupils dilated, "Is this the black soil in contact with the World Seal at the very bottom?!"

"Indeed."

This meant Lu Yang had just dived from the surface straight to the deepest part of the ground in one breath!

"After I performed Earth Shrinking, I still uncontrollably activated the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype. Compared to usual, my burrowing speed this time was very fast, so fast that not even the king of earthworms, the Silver Ring King Worm, could catch up with me!"

Lu Yang was quite proud; it meant he was unmatched in the art of burrowing!

"Shrink Inch."

Lu Yang used Shrink Inch and turned into a tiny figure an inch tall.

Yun Mengmeng, seeing such a tiny Lu Yang for the first time, found it very amusing and poked him.

Lu Yang felt that this image compromised the dignity of the Second-in-command, so he gradually expanded from one inch to three inches, then to one zhang and three zhangs... eventually becoming a giant a thousand zhang tall!

However, his growing process was like that of bamboo, growing section by section, increasing only inch by inch.

"Ha ha, now I can perform the true Law of Heaven and Earth!" Lu Yang laughed loudly, his voice resonant and loud enough to shatter the clouds in the sky.

Soon after, Lu Yang returned to his original size.

"How do you explain ’Shrinking Ground Into Inches?’" Meng Jingzhou was a bit convinced by Lu Yang’s previous nonsensical explanations,

mainly because he couldn’t think of any other explanation.

Neither Fairy Eternity nor Yun Mengmeng answered; at a critical moment, Lu Yang reliably provided an explanation.

"It’s easy to explain. Just now I grew in increments of ’inches,’ which suggests that ’Shrink Inch’ might have been used for standardized measurements during Ancient Times. ’Earth Shrinking’? Literally, it means shrinking into the earth."

Meng Jingzhou still didn’t understand, "How did two unrelated words end up being put together?"

"’Even if I have to dig three feet into the ground, I will find him.’ Does that sound familiar?"

Meng Jingzhou nodded; who hasn’t heard this saying?

"Fairy, have you heard this saying during Ancient Times?"

"I have."

Lu Yang stroked his chin, speculating, "According to my guess, this saying existed in Ancient Times."

"For example, if something was stolen from the tribe, the Clan Leader would order the entire clan to find it, even if it meant digging three feet into the ground."

"But how much is three feet? It’s hard to say, so our ancestors would become thirty inches tall, which is three feet tall, then shrink into the ground. Now, wouldn’t that show exactly how much three feet deep is?"

Meng Jingzhou, "..."

Old Lu, I wanted to believe you, but could your explanation be a bit more plausible?

Lu Yang thought more and more that his guess was correct, "Think about it, from a literal standpoint, does ’Shrinking Ground Into Inches’ sound like a Space Spell? Definitely not."

"The ancient ancestors had only recently created writing, so the magic skills they employed surely had to be understood from their literal meaning. It was later generations that misunderstood this skill."

"Don’t believe me? Ask Junior Brother Man Gu. When he first saw the term ’Shrinking Ground Into Inches,’ did he think it was a Space Spell?"

Then, Lu Yang performed ’Heaven and Earth in an Inch,’ disappearing from his spot.

When Lu Yang returned, he held sand from Buddha Country, snow from the Extreme Northern Lands, seawater from East Sea... proving how many places he had visited in just a short time.

Before, when Lu Yang used ’Heaven and Earth in an Inch,’ he would be randomly transported to a cliffside, but after activating the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype, the result was quite different—he could finally choose which cliffside to appear on.

"So what does this prove? This proves the magic skills I learned are the correct ones, and all of you have been learning them wrong," Lu Yang crossed his arms, looking as smug as could be, having waited so long for this day of vindication after being slandered for so long.

I knew my magic talent was so good; how could I possibly learn incorrectly?

After such a lengthy spiel, Meng Jingzhou suspected that the last sentence was really what Old Lu wanted to say.

Chapter 1270: 1269: Bipedal Golden Crow

As a good brother, Meng Jingzhou felt it was necessary to comfort Lu Yang.

"Old Lu, keep it normal. Everyone knows about your magical talent. Although you often learn spells in a slightly odd way, we all praise you as a magic genius when we’re outside. We’ve never talked about how you learn them crookedly. Don’t put too much pressure on yourself."

"I’m quite normal, look at me. The spells I learn correctly are ’Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth’ and the ’Playing Dead Technique.’"

"The ’Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth,’ a Divine Ability inherent to the Taotie Clan, wasn’t originally about devouring everything but was capable of consuming Spiritual Energy and soil."

"Right, this Immortal can testify to that." Fairy Eternity said, as Lu Yang’s ’Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth’ was taught by her.

"And the ’Playing Dead Technique’ is exactly as the Fairy taught."

"See, the spells that I’ve learned without twisting them are just the true forms of the spells."

Meng Jingzhou let out a long sigh. When put that way, it did seem to make sense.

So, the spells that are now circulating are not necessarily in their original forms.

And Old Lu’s ’Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype’ could trace back to the origin and restore spells to their original state?

"Why don’t we ask Eldest Senior Sister about it?"

"Sure."

This was a good opportunity to share the great news about successfully forming the Dao Fruit Outline with Eldest Senior Sister.

After repairing the lake, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou went to see their Eldest Senior Sister.

The Immortal Lineage, along with Meng Jingzhou, arrived at the Eldest Senior Sister’s Abode. The door opened, and Eldest Senior Sister was inside, drinking tea and reading.

Lu Yang noticed that the teaware Eldest Senior Sister was using was the very set he had gifted her.

"What’s the matter?"

Eldest Senior Sister put down her teacup and gestured for everyone to take a seat.

Yun Mengmeng, carrying a small stool, sat down next to Yun Zhi, excitedly gesticulating, "Xiao Zhi, let me tell you. The Second-in-command is so powerful now, he’s become a Semi-Immortal, absolutely invincible among his peers. Chief Housekeeper even praised his Dao Fruit Outline for comprehending the essence of the Invincible Elixir. It’s a pity I met Chief Housekeeper too late to cultivate my own Invincible Elixir..."

Yun Zhi pulled out a cookie from beside the table and stuffed it into her good sister’s mouth to silence her.

"Junior Brother, you talk."

"Eldest Senior Sister, I have condensed a Dao Fruit Outline. I named it the ’Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype,’ which can trace things back to their origins, allowing them to revert to their most primal state. But since it just formed, its power is not enough yet. It could only revert Old Meng from Middle Tribulation Stage back to Early Tribulation Stage."

Meng Jingzhou glared at Lu Yang. Just talk about your Dao Fruit Prototype, why bring me into it!

Yun Zhi’s body trembled slightly as she realized the intention behind her Junior Brother forming the ’Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype.’

If Junior Brother became an Immortal, he indeed might be able to pull her back from her Harmonization state.

Yun Zhi felt somewhat guilty. As Eldest Senior Sister, she should’ve been shielding her Junior Brother from storms, yet now, it was him who worried for her.

Her gaze softened a touch.

"However, when using the Dao Fruit Prototype, I ran into a little trouble." Lu Yang felt a bit embarrassed. Even though he had become a Semi-Immortal, he still had to seek advice from Eldest Senior Sister.

"What’s the problem?"

Lu Yang recounted the entire incident along with his speculation.

After he finished speaking, even Yun Zhi, who was usually devoid of expression, seemed to have a twitch at the corner of her eyes.

Junior Brother’s speculation about the actions of our ancient forebearers was quite far-fetched. But on further consideration, it wasn’t unreasonable.

"Unfortunately, I can’t explain this problem."

Yun Zhi knew far less about history than Fairy Eternity, an ancient being. If Fairy Eternity couldn’t confirm the validity of Lu Yang’s hypothesis, how could she be sure?

"It would be nice to confirm some guess."

All of a sudden, Fairy Eternity’s eyes lit up, "Oh, right! Immortal Jiuchong once told me that the Golden Crow race originally had two legs!"

With that, Lu Yang also remembered. When he first learned the ’Golden Bird True Flame,’ Fairy had mentioned this fact.

It seemed necessary to seek out the presence of Immortal Jiuchong.

"Where is Immortal Jiuchong?"

"In the Imperial City," Yun Zhi said.

"The preparations for the early stages of unsealing are complete; we only lack the aid of the Eternity Dao Fruit from Fairy Senior Sister. All four seniors are gathering in the Imperial City, and they asked me to please request Junior Brother to head to the Imperial City with Fairy Senior Sister after you leave seclusion."

"They specifically instructed me to ask you to make sure to attend."

Lu Yang was flattered, unable to believe he held such importance among the Four Ancient Immortals.

"They said you must properly report to them the great feat of unsealing."

Lu Yang: "..."

He had overestimated himself.

"Let’s go; I’ll accompany you," Yun Zhi said as she packed up the tea set, realizing that missing such a historic moment would be a regret.

Yun Zhi, Immortal Fairy, Yun Mengmeng, Lu Yang, and Meng Jingzhou—five people set off for the Imperial City together.

With Yun Zhi leading the way, the group quickly arrived at the Imperial City.

Upon reaching the Imperial City, they learned from Emperor Xia that although the Four Ancient Immortals were in the city, they weren’t all together, each enjoying their own life.

For example, Immortal Jiuchong was teaching at Taixue Academy.

Taixue Academy was a place specially dedicated to the study of history, where all the historical scholars of Great Xia gathered.

Re-entering Taixue Academy, Lu Yang felt sentimental: "Speaking of which, the last principal of Taixue Academy was Guan Shanhai."

When everyone found Immortal Jiuchong, he was in the lecture hall discussing historical issues with scholars. However, the people of Taixue Academy were unaware of Jiuchong’s true identity, just treating him as a teacher with a notable background.

"I’m telling you, among the Four Ancient Immortals, Jiuchong is definitely the strongest. This is historically supported. Look, this is the Immortal’s Notes from Immortal Jiuchong; it couldn’t be faked!"

"Indeed, it is Jiuchong’s handwriting, but why does this book look so new?"

Immortal Jiuchong inwardly cursed his luck, realizing he forgot to have Time Immortal age it.

Yun Zhi originally wanted to invite Immortal Jiuchong out, but Lu Yang stopped her.

"Eldest Senior Sister, perhaps it’s best not to do this. If Immortal Jiuchong is hiding his name here, he must not wish to reveal his identity. If we barge in recklessly, wouldn’t we expose him?"

Yun Zhi nodded, appreciating the Junior Brother’s thorough consideration, and opted to send a message asking Immortal Jiuchong to come out for a chat.

As Immortal Jiuchong was arguing with someone, he heard Yun Zhi’s message, left the lecture hall, and was stunned to see everyone—filled with regret.

"Ah, how come you all are here? If you were coming, you should have entered the lecture hall and said, ’Immortal Jiuchong, it’s time; please unseal the World.’"

"That way, the whole academy would know my identity!"

"..."

"Senior, since you wish to reveal your identity, why stay hidden here?" asked Lu Yang.

"It’s one thing to reveal my identity on my own, another for others to uncover it!"

"That makes sense."

Immortal Fairy cut off Jiuchong’s sharing of wisdom, asking, "Jiuchong, didn’t you say before that the Golden Crow species originally had two legs?"

Immortal Jiuchong, puzzled, wondered when the Immortal Fairy became so interested in history.

"Yes, in ancient times the Golden Crow species only had two legs. Later on, as the ancient predecessors grew stronger and heard that Golden Crows could invigorate Yang, they began hunting them. To survive, Golden Crows opted for evolution, gradually developing into three-legged creatures."

"The gecko’s tail regrowth for survival was learned from the Golden Crows."

"As for what they looked like, it was like this."

Immortal Jiuchong raised his hand, conjuring a lifelike, radiant two-legged Golden Bird.

It was exactly like Lu Yang’s Golden Bird True Flame.

Chapter 1271: 1270: Difficult to Explain Predictions of the Future

Indeed, are all the magic skills one learns the original forms of those skills?

The words of Immortal Jiuchong made Lu Yang more convinced of his assumption.

"What was the Samadhi True Fire, Shrinking Ground Into Inches, and Heaven and Earth in an Inch like during ancient times?" Lu Yang asked.

Immortal Jiuchong was puzzled: "What what?"

"That is to say, were these magic skills different in the ancient times compared to now?"

Immortal Jiuchong found Lu Yang’s question somewhat amusing, but seeing everyone’s serious reactions, he didn’t dare to jump to conclusions lightly.

"It’s hard to say. There are too few records of ancient times. The ancient history that we know of today is what I have carefully distinguished from legends passed down from various races, determining which parts are true and which are exaggerated."

"And most legends are difficult to distinguish between truth and falsehood. For example, the now-extinct Kua Fu Clan left behind legends. The Kua Fu Clan was said to be giants, for whom ordinary clothes were insufficient to cover their bodies, hence they researched magic skills that could enlarge ordinary clothing, such as Imitation Fist and Law of Heaven and Earth, which are said to share the same origin."

"I see, thank you, Senior, for your information." Lu Yang thanked him with a fist and palm salute.

Immortal Jiuchong waved his hand: "These are just unsubstantiated legends. I used to take them as jokes and tell them to Ying Tian and the others, but they were not interested."

"Now that Little Friend Lu has come out of retreat, and Fairy Eternity has also arrived here, does that mean we can break the seal?"

Immortal Jiuchong was eager to try; for this day, he had made many preparations, ensuring it would be more spectacular than the phenomena accompanying the Crossing the Immortal Tribulation.

Just as Fairy Eternity was about to agree, Lu Yang interjected: "We still have some matters to confirm, and once confirmed, would it be alright for the Fairy to gather with the four seniors at the Imperial Palace?"

"That will be fine."

Seeing this, Immortal Jiuchong stopped arguing with the ignoramuses from Taixue Academy about who was the true leader of the Four Ancient Immortals and went to wait for everyone at the Imperial Palace.

After Immortal Jiuchong left, Fairy Eternity was puzzled: "Do we still have something to take care of?"

"Fairy, the ’predict the future’ skill you taught me, you learned it from Senior Sui Yue, right? I want to ask Senior Sui Yue how this skill came about."

According to Emperor Xia, Senior Sui Yue was currently at the headquarters of Land Money Trade Association.

Everyone left Taixue Academy and headed to the headquarters of Land Money Trade Association.

Apart from Yun Zhi, it was everyone’s first time at the headquarters of the association. True to its reputation as the premier trade association in the world, it was indeed extravagant. Two Pixiu statues carved from spirit stones stood guard at the entrance, the ground was paved with red Warm Jade which kept it warm in winter and cool in summer. The whole place was situated upon a giant Meditation Array Formation, which not only aided in cultivation but more importantly, helped maintain composure. Moreover, each floor was permanently guarded by cultivators in the Body Integration Realm, ensuring the headquarters’ utmost protection.

Those coming in and out were merchants with immense wealth, or cultivators above the Divinity Transformation Stage.

"Old Meng, have you never been here before?" Lu Yang noticed Meng Jingzhou surveying the area back and forth, just like a newcomer.

"I haven’t been here. The people from my family who come to the headquarters are here for business, and they can’t bring me along. After starting my cultivation, I have not returned to Imperial City often, and when I do, it’s to see my family, so there’s no need to come here."

"That makes sense."

Yun Mengmeng had an indifferent reaction: "This is the top trade association in the world? It doesn’t seem better than our Sky Gate Mountain."

She’d heard people say that the headquarters of Land Money Trade Association was full of worldly wealth and luxury, but now it seemed average to her. The items here were not as valuable as those at Sky Gate Mountain, nor were the people’s cultivation levels as high.

"Where is Senior Sui Yue?"

"He should be on the top floor," Yun Zhi said, guessing why Senior Sui Yue was there and leading everyone upstairs.

Ordinarily, the upper levels of the headquarters were off-limits to outsiders, but Yun Zhi’s distant and icy aura was so strong that anyone wishing to stop them was too scared to move.

An attendant mustered the courage to try to stop them but was held back by the supervisor.

"That’s Yun Zhi. There’s no need to stop her."

"Yun Zhi?" The attendant was quite unfamiliar with this name.

"Yun Zhi is the Eldest Senior Sister of the Seek Dao Sect and also an important customer of our association. The Chairman specifically instructed that she may come and go in the headquarters at will and must not be obstructed."

"Can it really be like that?"

"It’s normal for you not to know. She hasn’t visited here for over a hundred years."

Yun Zhi led everyone to the Chairman’s room at the end of the top floor.

Before she could knock, the door automatically opened. The Time Immortal was sitting in the Chairman’s seat, teaching, with two students in front of him.

One of the students was someone Lu Yang recognized – the Thief King.

The other student was an old man with only one leg, who must be the Thief Ancestor.

The Thief King shuddered involuntarily when he saw Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, recalling the terrifying events in Zhongshan County.

"Senior is teaching?" Yun Zhi asked.

The Time Immortal stood up with a chuckle. "I’ve got some fate with them in the realm of cultivation, so I simply shared some lessons. Alright, I have some matters to attend to, you all should head down first."

The Thief King and the Thief Ancestor bowed respectfully: "Understood."

Both of them had condensed the outline of the Speed Dao Fruit, and they yearned for the Time Dao Fruit, an Upper Dao Fruit. Knowing the appearance of Senior Sui Yue in the Imperial City, they had earnestly requested teachings from the Time Immortal.

Yun Zhi nodded; she had guessed as much. For the Time Immortal to appear in the headquarters of the association, it could only be related to the Thief King and Thief Ancestor.

"Little Friend Lu has come out of retreat? It seems you’ve obtained quite a harvest? Is it to break the World Seal?"

Lu Yang bowed with his fist: "Last time, thank you for the teachings, Senior Sui Yue. I’ve come this time, firstly to break the seal, and secondly, to seek clarification on something unclear."

"Oh? What’s the matter?"

"Did Senior once teach a Divine Ability called ’predict the future’ to the Fairy Eternity? Is it a magic skill you created?"

"It would be more accurate to say it’s a magic skill I was born with rather than one I created."

"Born with?"

Everyone was surprised, including the Immortal Fairy, who thought that this ability was a result of the Time Immortal’s understanding of the Time Dao Fruit.

"It’s somewhat similar to the Divine Abilities inherent to the Demon Race due to their bloodlines."

"You know, my true body is the Firmament Tree. After I gained Spiritual Wisdom, I felt as though I knew a magic skill innately. As my Spiritual Wisdom improved, the skill became clearer. After trying to use it, I discovered it could foresee future events, so I named this ability ’predict the future.’"

The Time Immortal’s reply was beyond Lu Yang’s expectation; he had thought it was a skill the Immortal had learned from somewhere else.

Since it was innate, there should be no confusion about it.

However, if that was the case, why was he able to exhibit ’predict the future,’ which could view time lines?

Suddenly, a thought struck Lu Yang as he remembered something. "By the way, Senior, did you mention that the Firmament Tree had faced a great calamity in the past, was severed in the middle, and was utterly lifeless, and you grew as a new Firmament Tree from the old one?"

"That’s right."

"Do you know what the great calamity was?"

The Time Immortal shook his head: "I do not know. I hadn’t yet gained Spiritual Wisdom at the time; it’s just a conjecture I made after I was born with Spiritual Wisdom based on the condition of my body."

Lu Yang frowned, the innate ’predict the future’ skill, the Firmament Tree being severed at its waist, and his display of a completely different ’predict the future.’

Was there a relationship between these three?

As Lu Yang pondered, a pair of eyes gazed at him in the shadows, but no one noticed.

Chapter 1272: 1271: Unsealing the Seal

A pair of eyes, shadowy and deep, fixed upon Lu Yang, yet no one perceived his presence; it was as if he existed not in this world, untouched by the myriad causality.

"Once again a clue about me has been found; such a troublesome lad. Should I kill him to prevent future troubles?"

Though at this moment many Immortals have convened in the Imperial City, this does not rule out making a move.

Or should I alter the timeline, changing reality?

The person peered at Lu Yang, pondering for a long time, ultimately abandoning both decisions.

No, this won’t do, the Box World is about to be opened; acting now would surely be an effort in vain.

It took great effort to make them believe I am Yao Saint, they think I’m already dead. Even if Lu Yang finds this matter strange, he shouldn’t suspect me.

Indeed, in their eyes, this matter has already concluded, everything returning to tranquility. There’s no need to expose myself over such trivial suspicions.

Besides, although Lu Yang is a difficult one, his experiences are quite intriguing; I really don’t want to make a move.

The only remaining concern is, where exactly could that reincarnated person be?

Well, if I can’t find him, others likely can’t either; it’s not really a concern.

That person’s gaze fell once again upon Lu Yang, his expression somewhat peculiar.

Speaking of which, this lad Lu Yang is truly good at guessing; his conjecture regarding the origin of the magic skill was almost entirely accurate.

...

Lu Yang pondered for a long while, then looked up with a relieved smile: "I can’t figure it out, so I won’t think about it. Let’s not waste time here, let’s go to the Imperial Palace."

"To the Imperial Palace?" Time Immortal asked in confusion.

"Mm, I’m thinking of requesting the four seniors to meet in the Imperial Palace, would that be alright?"

"Are we going to unlock the seal?" Time Immortal rubbed his hands together eagerly, finally, the day had come.

Fairy Eternity also smiled to everyone, "None of you have seen the real starry sky, right? Today, I’ll let you feast your eyes!"

Meng Jingzhou and Yun Mengmeng were both full of anticipation, especially Yun Mengmeng, who felt that leaving the Secret Realm to follow Xiao Zhi was the best decision of her life, always encountering novelties.

The group arrived at the Daming Hall in the Imperial Palace, with only Emperor Xia, Immortal Jiuchong, Jiang Pingan, and Meng Junzi present.

"Elder Ancestor," Meng Jingzhou was especially respectful in front of Meng Junzi.

"Good lad, reached Middle Tribulation Stage, you haven’t disappointed your old ancestor!" Meng Junzi laughed heartily, Meng Jingzhou always bringing him surprises.

Just a few years, and already reaching Middle Tribulation Stage.

"My junior has made a breakthrough, Old Jiang, as the elder, shouldn’t you show some gesture?" Meng Junzi wrapped his arm around Meng Jingzhou, boasting in front of Jiang Pingan.

"Why not lend him the Taishang Sword to play with for a couple of days?"

Jiang Pingan was so angry that his eyes bulged: "I’ll give you a taste of my sword that’s more like it!"

"Don’t be so stingy, if not the Taishang Sword, handing over the Imperial Jade Seal is also fine."

Jiang Pingan, unable to contain himself any longer, lifted the Imperial Jade Seal to smash it at Meng Junzi, naturally, Meng Junzi nimbly dodged it.

"So this is the throne." Yun Mengmeng tapped around the throne above the temple grounds.

"It doesn’t seem comfortable to sit on, not as comfy as Xiao Zhi’s chair."

Her gaze towards Emperor Xia carried a touch of sympathy: "Being an emperor is indeed suffering."

"Turns out you went to find Suiyue." Immortal Jiuchong smiled, "We’ve already sent someone to inform Kirin and Ying Tian, they should be arriving soon."

"Haha, I heard someone talking about me the moment I stood at the entrance." Yingtian Immortal laughed heartily as he walked into the Daming Hall, looking around, "I was worried I’d be the last to arrive, but it seems Kirin is the last one."

Ao Ling and the others greeted Fairy Eternity Lu Yang with cheerful smiles.

"Since Elder Brother Lu Yang is here, does that mean you’ve successfully condensed the outline of a Dao Fruit? Congratulations, congratulations."

"Of course. Who do you think has fostered Little Yang? Fairy Eternity boasts proudly: "Little Yang has perfectly understood the Invincible Elixir before condensing the Dao Fruit outline, which is much stronger than the two of you."

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi smiled awkwardly: "Otherwise, why would we say Elder Brother Lu Yang? Us two Junior Sisters certainly can’t compare."

"The two seniors are too flattering..."

Ao Ling pretended to be angry: "You’re calling us seniors even though you’re almost a Semi-Immortal?"

Left with no choice, Lu Yang had to steel himself and change his form of address: "The two Senior Sisters... Junior Sister is too flattering."

Hearing Lu Yang calling them Junior Sister, Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi turned their anger into joy.

"Let’s not talk about this anymore. Now that everyone’s here, shall the seniors start to take action?" Lu Yang awkwardly changed the subject.

"Shall we start?" said Yingtian Immortal.

"Let’s go."

...

"Look quick, there’s some commotion at the Imperial Palace!"

Outside the Imperial Palace, a keen Cultivator was the first to sense the anomaly at the palace.

Above the palace stood four towering figures with differing forms: one bathed in lightning like a chosen one of heaven, one semi-kneeling with a fist slamming to the ground, one with arms crossed and a resolute gaze, and one sitting cross-legged in midair murmuring incantations.

Four figures, four forms, four auras. Each stood as an independent entity, yet together they exuded a towering and unbreakable presence.

"It’s the Four Ancient Immortals!"

People in the Imperial City excitedly pointed at those four figures, never expecting the Four Ancient Immortals to appear in the Imperial City at once.

How many people will never in their lifetime see an Immortal, yet they were fortunate to see four at once!

"What are they going to do?" Those Integration Period Cultivators thought of more possibilities, filled with surging emotions.

"Could they be unsealing the seal?"

"Definitely!"

The Integration Period Cultivators saw joy in each other’s eyes, not knowing what the outside world was like.

Yingtian Immortal slowly raised his head, his gaze profound, piercing through endless space, looking towards the Land of No Spirit above, his voice ancient, with a pause in between.

"Has the time, arrived?"

The Qilin Immortal stood up from his semi-kneel, recounting a past unknown to the people: "Who would have thought, a battle from the past, the supreme power of extremity, led to this seal lasting for three hundred thousand years."

Immortal Jiuchong, with resolve in his eyes, also looked towards the seal: "With the fall of a formidable enemy, the order that stirred the ages is set right, and this seal should also disappear."

Time Immortal stopped reciting the scriptures, akin to the big brother among the four, his voice not loud but filled with authority, making one feel it was beyond question.
