Zhan Po shook his head: "The most challenging aspect of dealing with Mutant Beasts is their organized actions. With the King of Mutant Beasts dead, we humans have the chance to break them one by one."

Zhan Po’s tone was decisive, his expression full of longing and expectation: "Ten years, at most ten years, and we humans will be able to reclaim Sky Sea Star, which rightfully belongs to us!"

The Salvation Team imagined the future and couldn’t help but smile.

Lu Yang also smiled and said, "Ten years is too long. Let me give you a hand."

Before the Salvation Team could react, Lu Yang stood up, drew out his Green Peak Sword, and with their jaws dropped in astonishment, he lifted off the ground and took flight.

Even the ever-talkative Yuan Xing was rendered speechless at the moment.

"Captain, can people actually fly?" Shi Lingyun nudged the captain. He had evolved to the utmost, so why couldn’t he fly?

And he didn’t have any wings, so how could he fly?

"Don’t ask me, I don’t know either."

Lu Yang soared through the clouds, arriving above Sky Sea Star, his Spiritual Sense spread out, covering the entire star, locking onto all Mutant Beasts.

Every Mutant Beast on this star was under Lu Yang’s full surveillance—the Beast Tide attacking cities, soldiers fighting to the death, and generals sharing their fate with their city.

Three of the top ten masters among the humans were among them, resisting the Beast Tide.

Even though the big tiger was dead and the Mutant Beasts had lost control, their bloodlust had been ignited in battle, and even without someone to control them, they would still attack the cities.

Mutant Beasts weren’t only on land but also in the sea, and there were several in the ocean on par with the big tiger, as well as one more powerful than the big tiger.

Perhaps that one in the ocean was the real King of Mutant Beasts; only the human elites and the Salvation Team were unaware of its existence.

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand."

The Green Peak Sword traced an arc in the sky, leaving behind an indelible sword image that spread like wildfire at an incredible speed.

If someone were in outer space, they would see Sky Sea Star coated in a golden sheen—that was the gleaming light of countless Green Peak Swords.

Using his index and middle fingers together, the immense Spiritual Sense locked onto every Mutant Beast, a sword image hovering above each of their heads.

He simply exhaled one word.

"Fall."

Sword images like scattered light, raining down like swords, the sword shadows acting like divine punishment, indiscriminately piercing through every bloodthirsty Mutant Beast.

No matter if it was the ordinary Mutant Beasts on land or the true King of Mutant Beasts hidden in the deep sea, each required only one sword image.

Feeling the threat to its life, the King of Mutant Beasts in the ocean tried desperately to escape. However, golden sword shadows descended from the heavens, plunging into the sea, and instead of slowing down, their speed increased even more.

The King of Mutant Beasts twisted its tentacles to fend off the attack.

Boom—

A mist of blood spread, and this King of Mutant Beasts, having not yet wreaked havoc in the human world, died at the bottom of the sea never reached by humans.

The Salvation Team did not know what Lu Yang, soaring through the sky, was planning to do. They were in the territory of the great tiger, with no mutant beasts nearby. All they could see was a distant descent of golden light.

Then, a hissing noise came through their communication channel, from the three Masters tasked with defending the city.

"Zhan Po, have you succeeded!?"

"The King of Mutant Beasts has been killed by you!"

"How did you do it? Swords, so many golden swords descended from the sky, killing all the mutant beasts!"

The city-defending human Masters spoke with an excited and incoherent tone. Knowing that the Salvation Team had gone to kill the King of Mutant Beasts, they naturally connected this event with the Salvation Team.

The Salvation Team looked at each other, shock and fear in their eyes.

What kind of method was this?!

Lu Yang landed with a smile, sheathing his Green Peak Sword: "Done."

"Mr. Lu..."

Lu Yang raised his hand to interrupt Shi Lingyun: "Let’s talk while we walk. Along the way, tell me your stories and show me around your city. If I could meet your President, that would be even better."

"That’s a given, that’s a given," Shi Lingyun hurriedly said. Even if Mr. Lu didn’t mention it, the President would take the initiative to meet him.

Seeing that Zhan Po was about to speak but hesitated, glancing at the great tiger’s corpse, Lu Yang understood what he meant.

He used the Identity Jade to collect the great tiger’s body. The mountain-like corpse shrank and vanished, once again astonishing the Salvation Team.

On the road, Lu Yang had the members of the Salvation Team recount their pasts in turn. Lu Yang simply listened, occasionally interrupting with a question or two. After clarifying their doubts, he would let them continue with their stories.

When the last person finished their story, Lu Yang seemed to smile wistfully:

"Each of you had different starting points and experiences, and then you formed the Salvation Team. You were still in sync until the decisive battle, becoming one whole. In the subsequent battle against the King of Mutant Beasts, Shi Lingyun, you made a breakthrough on the spot, defeating the King of Mutant Beasts, and became a hero of the human race. Brilliant, very brilliant."

Lu Yang applauded, seeming to cheer for their life experiences, leaving Zhan Po and the others puzzled. They felt that Mr. Lu was praising them, but the tone seemed off.

Lu Yang took out the great tiger’s corpse in advance and let the Salvation Team carry it as their trophy, allowing them to show off in front of the public.

When they reached the city, the city walls were stained with blood, having endured countless bloody battles.

By now, the news of the Salvation Team’s defeat of the King of Mutant Beasts had spread, and the city gates were wide open, with the excited populace welcoming the heroes back in triumph.

The President came out to meet them in person.

The President was an old man around sixty years old, the former leader of the Top Ten Masters. He warmly embraced Zhan Po and the others.

"Zhan Po, Shi Lingyun, and all of you, I knew you could do it!"

When he came to Lu Yang and met his dark eyes, feeling as if all his secrets were seen through, the President hesitated for a moment before choosing to come forward and shake hands.

"This must be the alien Mr. Lu, thank you so much for saving us."

"This is not a place for an extended conversation. Let’s talk in my office," the President invited the Salvation Team and the alien Mr. Lu to the core area of the city.

In the office, the President personally served tea and water to everyone, showing great people skills.

Lu Yang took the cup of tea from the President, expressed his thanks, and then casually asked, "By the way, Mr. President, what are our surveillance ratings?"

Chapter 1284: 1283: Isn’t the New Character Unreasonably Strong?

"Ah, Mr. Lu, what are you saying?" The president feigned confusion, pretending not to understand what Lu Yang was talking about.

The Salvation Team and others truly did not understand Lu Yang’s words.

Lu Yang held his teacup, blowing away the steam: "Don’t understand, huh? Then let me change the question. Was the experiment conducted by the mad scientist that created Mutant Beasts thirty years ago really just an accident?"

"Mr. Lu, what do you mean by that?" Shi Lingyun felt that Mr. Lu’s question was bizarre, yet he always felt there was some connection between the two questions.

Lu Yang stood up and paced in front of the Salvation Team: "Lingyun, you come from humble beginnings and rose from the bottom because of your special physique. You have made rapid progress along the way, winning the Beauty’s hand, and though you’ve had troubles, you’ve always resolved them. Do you find your life incredibly thrilling?"

"Zhan Po, as the world’s top expert, you’ve experienced many ups and downs to attain your status today. Silent and reserved but respected by all, I’d imagine your image is quite popular."

"Tianxing, you come from a second-generation influential family but stick to your own convictions..."

"Yuan Xing, you mask your yearning for your wife with laughter..."

"Your stories behind the scenes are truly wonderful."

"Of course, the most wonderful is the director’s design."

...

"Shi Lingyun, having defeated the Weapon Expert, finally became the second-in-command recognized by the Salvation Team. With all members of the Salvation Team assembled, the seven Saviors challenge the King of Mutant Beasts. As for what the outcome will be, stay tuned for tonight’s ’Sky Sea Star Legend’!"

The preview for "Sky Sea Star Legend" played continuously on the big screen.

"Sky Sea Star Legend" is a hit TV series of the Interstellar Alliance, with scenes so realistic that even the most professional special effects experts could find no flaws.

With the impressive acting skills of the actors, unpredictable and tradition-defying plots, and ruthless when it comes to thrilling scenes, it rightfully holds the crown of TV series, consistently keeping a 20 percent viewership rating and a first-place lead.

For the Interstellar Alliance, with its vast array of entertainment options, this is nothing short of a myth.

The drama production is making a killing—just one advertisement slot costs a fortune, and merchandise is in high demand. Each character has their own fan club.

The Salvation Team’s battle against the King of Mutant Beasts is indeed the climax of the series, with conservative estimates predicting viewership could reach 30 percent.

"Who do you think will be the highlight this time? Will the cold-faced sword god Zhan Po make his breakthrough?"

"Hard to say, I think it’s Shi Lingyun. The scene where he absorbs the Enhancer hints that he might fully assimilate it during the battle."

"It might not yet be Shi Lingyun’s shining moment. Some big shot analyzed that the real King of Mutant Beasts is in the ocean—that will be Lingyun’s true opponent!"

Posts about "Sky Sea Star Legend" keep popping up online, the excitement of the audience palpable through the screen.

The director is busily organizing the drama team.

"Are the Nano Cameras adjusted properly?"

"Is the quantum communication channel clear?"

"Can we ensure that Shi Lingyun’s monitoring data is retrieved, so that he can break through during the battle?"

At this moment, the director is ecstatic.

Through "Sky Sea Star Legend," he has already become the undisputed number one director of the Alliance, welcomed wherever he goes, with the financial elite and influential families treating him as an equal.

But he is an artist with aspirations that go beyond this success—he believes tonight will be the pinnacle of his life!

He found the original, undiscovered planet Sky Sea Star, and by inputting everything about it into a quantum computer, he could predict the development of this planet. All he needed was to introduce a few variables based on the quantum computer’s results, and Sky Sea Star would change according to his wishes, making it flawless even in a live broadcast.

To him, money and profits are unimportant. Being labeled as a human trafficker for selling people, or reporting a new planet to the Alliance, he might at best receive an "Alliance Medal," which is so common you wouldn’t know the recipients without actively searching online.

"Sky Sea Star Legend" is different—it is a drama that will make his name immortal, an insurmountable monument in the art world.

Sure, what he’s doing is illegal, but shouldn’t art be unrestricted by law?

"Are we all set? Let’s begin the broadcast!"

With the director’s command, the climax of the drama went live across the net.

Nano Cameras focused on the King of Mutant Beasts, and according to the director’s plan, the sequence was designed to capture the oppressiveness of the King of Mutant Beasts, creating a tense atmosphere like the calm before the storm.

Then, the audience of the whole Alliance saw a traditional Sword Cultivator effortlessly pierce through the King of Mutant Beasts’ forehead, the entire process casually executed.

Director: "?"

Who are you?

The comments section and the bullet chat instantly exploded.

"Who’s this guy, appearing so abruptly!"

"Why is the costume and makeup department so careless, who in the apocalypse is spotless and still wearing ancient attire?"

"Is this a new character, how come there’s no foreshadowing at all?"

"This is the essence of the show, you can never predict what will happen next scene!"

"That kind of makes sense."

"Do you guys think this new character is handsome, like a carefree and casual swordsman?"

"Wait for the spin-off, he must have an exciting past."

From the audience’s comments, this new character was very popular, but the director didn’t think so.

Right now he felt like ants on a hot pan, he was having the quantum computer calculate this person’s actions.

"Hope nothing happens."

Then the Salvation Team and the new character clashed; the new character easily defeated the Salvation Team, and even Shi Lingyun’s breakthrough at the last moment was useless.

The audience could feel the despair and helplessness through the screen.

Later, the misunderstanding was cleared up, nano cameras covered the entire planet; even if Lu Yang flew to the sky, everything was captured clearly.

The Interstellar Alliance audience saw the new character, One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand, annihilated all the Mutant Beasts in one fell swoop.

"This special effect is so realistic, my home quantum computer would smoke trying to render such lifelike effects!"

"This person could probably rival the starfleet."

"Comparing virtual characters to real combat strength again."

"Do you guys think he could be an extraterrestrial visitor?"

"Can’t be, right? Introducing a new map near the end of the show?"

"Isn’t this new character ridiculously strong?"

"I get it, this kind of invincible character is definitely for plot progression, otherwise, there’s no way to continue the story."

The emergence of the new character brought a completely different experience to the audience; the viewership ratings shot up to 40 percent!

The director was in a daze, and the quantum computer only managed to conclude "extraterrestrial visitor" before smoking and giving up.

"Director, what should we do?" The cameraman and others looked at each other, they all signed confidentiality agreements, surely they wouldn’t leak the truth, but how should the story continue now?

The director snapped out of it, now was not the time to sit and accept his fate: "Send a message to the president, ask him to stall this person, gather as much intelligence as we can!"

"The nano cameras are less than one nanometer in size, this person definitely hasn’t noticed us yet!"

The president is part of the production team, all variables on Sky Sea Star are managed by the president.

At this moment, Lu Yang led the Salvation Team back in triumph; the president welcomed them warmly and ushered them into the office.

Just as the director heaved a sigh of relief, putting a cold compress on his face to cool down and plan the next steps, he saw on the big screen that Lu Yang was reviewing the Salvation Team’s past exploits.

"Your behind-the-scenes stories are really fascinating."

"Of course, the most fascinating of all is the director’s design."

Suddenly, Lu Yang turned his head, his face filling the entire big screen, talking to the director through the camera.

"You think so too, right, director?"

The audience was astounded, and the director felt like he had fallen into an ice cave.

Chapter 1285: 1284: Director’s Jinx

The plot has gone haywire, and the comment section of "Sky Sea Star Legend" was frantically abuzz. Viewers had completely no idea how the TV show’s story was going to unfold next.

Before airing, all those long-winded dissections and analyses of the plot from various experts were deemed useless.

"What’s going on, does this new character know they’re in a TV show?"

"Can some experts come out and analyze this? Has this absurd new character really never appeared before?"

"I dare someone to say ’Sky Sea Star Legend’ is predictable and boring now, like you could guess what’s going to happen next, like it’s either Zhan Po leveling up to deal with the King of Mutant Beasts or Shi Lingyun breaking through to deal with the King of Mutant Beasts!"

Viewers excitedly typed their thoughts; it was just too thrilling, today’s plot was simply too stimulating!

The director and the crew felt as if a bucket of ice water had been poured over them, chilling them to the bone.

"How did he discover the camera!?"

There’s no way the nano cameras could be distinguished by the human eye.

The director, as the main pillar of the team, was the first to calm down: "He must be carrying some kind of scanner that detected the cameras."

"Don’t panic, everyone. He can only talk to us through the camera; he doesn’t know where we are!"

"Even President Ma Jie doesn’t know the location of Sky Sea Star within the Interstellar Alliance, so even if they capture President Ma, it’s useless!"

Hearing this, the crew gradually calmed down, realizing the rationale.

...

"Mr. President, do you have anything you’d like to say, like who’s really behind you, and where they might be?" Lu Yang gulped down his tea, set down the cup, savoring the taste which was mediocre, as was its toxicity.

The faces of the seven members of the Salvation Team went pale; as ten top experts, they weren’t just peak performers in combat but also far smarter than average. Even though Lu Yang hadn’t spelled out the situation, they understood his meaning through just a few words.

The livid expression on the President’s face confirmed their suspicions!

They just couldn’t figure out, if all this was for filming a TV show, then what was real and what was fake?

Were only the seven of them kept in the dark, or was the whole of Sky Sea Star fooled?!

What did everything they had experienced count for, a joke to amuse others?

"President Ma Jie, answer Mr. Lu’s questions!"

President Ma, whose face had been ashen and silent up to now, revealed a smile: "Mr. Lu, you really are an unexpected variable."

He pointed at his mouth with a ferocious expression: "I have a nuclear bomb remote and a locator in my teeth. I advise you to let me go, or once I bite down on the remote trigger, the nuclear bomb will launch right here, and then we’ll all be finished together!"

With the TV show’s secrets exposed, they absolutely couldn’t fall into the other party’s hands. Even if Lu Yang didn’t come after him, the entire population of Sky Sea Star could drown them with their spit alone!

President Ma, as the leader of the previous generation’s top ten experts, wasn’t just a scripted character. He actually had that level of strength, and not even Zhan Po and Shi Lingyun -- who had fully absorbed the Enhancer -- were a match for him.

But he also knew that no matter how strong he was, he couldn’t possibly be a match for Lu Yang, who could single-handedly deal with all of the Mutant Beasts. His only option now was to escape!

He had on him the highest-grade anti-tracking device and a signal jammer, so even if Lu Yang used a scanner, he wouldn’t be able to find him!

The Salvation Team and others were torn; if the nuclear bomb exploded here, everyone in the city would die, but if they simply let President Ma go, it would be hard to swallow!

Just as everyone was conflicted, President Ma dropped two smoke bombs from his cuffs to block everyone’s sight, and amidst the smoke, his laughter could be heard before it disappeared.

Smoke cleared, and everyone saw Lu Yang pin President Ma to the ground, leaving his teeth shattered across the floor.

Lu Yang raised an eyebrow: "Where would I put my face if you ran away?"

"This is impossible!"

President Ma was horrified, when he released the smoke bomb, he also created multiple fake signals. How could Lu Yang possibly find him?

Is their technology level so much higher?

"I can tell you who is backing me; I’m a citizen of the Interstellar Alliance, backed by the Alliance’s TV station. I advise you to release me. Otherwise, when the Interstellar Fleet comes, you’ll be the one to die!"

"Interstellar Alliance?" Lu Yang heard an interesting term, it sounded like a colossal space power, technically probably far superior to that of Sky Sea Star.

It would be great to transplant the Interstellar Alliance’s tech to the Cultivation World, for the benefit of the common people.

But President Ma Jie unwilling to cooperate in the interrogation was a bit troublesome. If he was subjected to a Soul Search, President Ma Jie would become an idiot, which would be too easy on him, and people of Sky Sea Star would have one less target for revenge.

Without a Soul Search, I wouldn’t know the location of the Interstellar Alliance.

Suddenly, Lu Yang noticed the Nano Cameras still filming, he could sense that the Nano Cameras were continuously sending some kind of signal, probably electromagnetic waves or some new term he hadn’t heard of, but that didn’t matter. What mattered was that the other end of the camera was probably connected to the Interstellar Alliance.

He pondered as he pinched one of the cameras, scaring the crew on the other end into a panic.

Soon commonsense prevailed over their emotions, there was no need for them to be afraid. Lu Yang could only talk one way, after all, he had no way to find them.

"Trace Origin."

Lu Yang spoke a word no one else understood, and the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype locked onto the Nano Camera, a line extending to the other end of the stars appeared in front of Lu Yang, visible only to him, not even for Immortal Fairy.

"Indeed, I can find the source of the camera."

Lu Yang found the Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype increasingly convenient to use.

Lu Yang kicked President Ma over to the Salvation Team: "This person is up to you. I’m off to visit this so-called Interstellar Alliance."

"Mr. Lu..."

Without even giving time for farewells, Lu Yang excitedly summoned the Black Canopy Boat. Jumping through space onboard, he followed the line extended by the camera toward the Interstellar Alliance.

There was no longer any threat to the Salvation Team on Sky Sea Star, so there was no need to worry about it for the time being. The situation with the Interstellar Alliance was unknown, and any delay could mean the crew escaping, which would be bad.

"What do we do, what do we do, is he coming over?!" The crew was in chaos, completely unable to comprehend Lu Yang’s methods.

Just how far had their technology developed, as if dealing with an immortal?!

The director took charge again: "Don’t panic, you all know how far Sky Sea Star is from the Alliance, and there are countless asteroid belts in between, he won’t fly over so quickly!"

"Let’s call the police quickly, or have the higher-ups deploy the military!"

Being caught by the Alliance government was far better than being seized by a mysterious and unpredictable alien. The director, pulling some strings, knew he wouldn’t face the death penalty, but who knows what would happen if caught by this alien.

"Exactly, an alien attack is like a terrorist attack, no, this is foreign intrusion, let the government protect us!"

Just as everyone was getting ready to contact the outside world for help, a boom was heard overhead, the rooftop was lifted off, and the promotional board of "Sky Sea Star Legend" that had been standing on the roof was placed before them, as a small boat glided down in the moonlight.

An anciently-dressed alien with hands in his robe descended upon the Interstellar Alliance in the Black Canopy Boat.

Chapter 1286: 1285: Your Servant Will Follow the Decree

Lu Yang was busy searching for the TV drama crew, merely glancing at the Interstellar Alliance, where the crew was located along the way.

Several colossal iron spheres hovered in the air, with one still under construction. Interstellar Spaceships ceaselessly shuttled back and forth, transporting goods. The planet where the crew was located was filled with skyscrapers and radiantly lit up, making it hard to distinguish whether it was day or night.

To be called the Interstellar Alliance, there must be more than one similar group of planets, and the degree of prosperity demonstrated the level of technological development of the Interstellar Alliance.

The investigation into the Interstellar Alliance could wait for another time; today’s main goal was to resolve the issue with the TV drama crew.

"It’s too much, they are all bad people, Little Yang, you can’t let them off." Fairy Eternity was fuming in the Spiritual Space. At first, she didn’t know what a TV drama was, nor did she understand what exactly happened in Sky Sea Star, but after Little Yang patiently explained it to her, she understood everything.

How could there be such bad people, controlling others’ lives and taking pleasure in it?

"Your servant obeys the command."

The moment the crew saw Lu Yang’s true form descend, they felt a chill envelop their limbs. What kind of method was this? How did he know the location of the Interstellar Alliance, and how did he bypass the asteroid belt in such a short time?

Of course, they didn’t know that Lu Yang had directed his Black Canopy Boat to head straight for the asteroid belt. A few small asteroids were no match for the Black Canopy Boat infused with Immortal Gold.

When the critical moment arrived, the director stepped forward to negotiate with Lu Yang.

"This esteemed alien visitor, there may be some misunderstandings between us. The development of the situation on Sky Sea Star was not our intention. The appearance of the Mutant Beasts was an accident, and we too wanted to help Sky Sea Star solve the problem with the Mutant Beasts, which is why we secretly trained the ten top masters. Our filming of the TV drama is merely to seek some insignificant benefits as a form of return for helping Sky Sea Star, that’s not too much to ask, is it?"

"Now that the Mutant Beasts problem has been resolved, we too are delighted for Sky Sea Star."

"I know you feel pity for the people of Sky Sea Star and want to help them, and we are the same in this respect. You are but one person alone, a lone tree cannot make a forest. Your backing forces must be far from Sky Sea Star, and it would be difficult even to offer support."

"We are very close to Sky Sea Star, we can send people over right now to help them rebuild their homes, and even lead them to join our Interstellar Alliance."

"Our Interstellar Alliance has twelve Warship Armies, powerful in strength. Your backing forces would surely be pleased to establish relations with us."

"If you are willing to meet with our Alliance’s high-level officials, I can introduce you, which will save you a lot of trouble. By then, you could be the diplomatic ambassador of your backing forces, leaving your name in history."

The director was convinced that the strength behind Lu Yang was a long distance from Sky Sea Star, and also far from the Alliance, otherwise, they would have discovered Sky Sea Star already.

This meant that Lu Yang was alone, without support, and certainly would not oppose them or the Alliance for the sake of Sky Sea Star.

Moreover, having promised him benefits and with such generous conditions, he believed that Lu Yang would not refuse.

In response, Lu Yang just dug at his ear and revealed an apologetic smile:

"Ah, sorry, I’m an alien, I don’t understand what you’re saying."

He blew lightly and directly sent the director flying onto the wall.

"Let’s fight him!"

Someone shouted,

The sound engineer swung a steel fist at Lu Yang. This punch could pierce through steel plates, but when it landed on Lu Yang, it didn’t even rip his clothes.

The sound engineer was about to pull back when Lu Yang, with a beaming smile, grabbed his arm and forcefully pulled, breaking his arm off.

Sparks flew.

The sound engineer’s two arms were actually mechanical arms!

"Interesting."

Lu Yang threw the arm to the side, and the broken arm accidentally hit the camera button, unexpectedly switching the view from Sky Sea Star to this scene!

The comment section and bullet screens were filled with question marks; ever since the new character appeared, the audience felt their brains were not enough to process it all.

"Can someone explain to me, what kind of plot twist is this?"

"The new character breaks through dimensions into reality? Creative director."

"That one hanging on the wall, is that the director? Doesn’t look like special effects, brutal!"

"Is the one with the severed hand the sound engineer? I’ve followed his account."

"Why does he have a mechanical arm?"

"Shows what you know, a sound engineer with a mechanical arm is totally normal. I bet his whole body is mechanical, except for his brain."

"Mechanical bodies with artificial neuron devices can transmit sensations back to the brain, so the touch of a mechanical body is indistinguishable from that of a flesh body."

"With a mechanical body, you’ll never get sick again, you can greatly extend your life span, change faces, and add custom features to suit your tastes – there are so many benefits, all the rich folks are doing it."

By the time Lu Yang had scanned President Ma Jie with his Spiritual Sense, he noticed that his body was mechanical, and his counter-tracking device and signal simulator were not carried on him, but installed within him.

After finding the crew, Lu Yang discovered that they, too, all had mechanical bodies.

The massive success of "Sky Sea Star Legend" transformed the crew members into tycoons who all modified their bodies.

"That’s good, no need to worry about accidentally breaking your arms or legs with too much force."

Thinking this, Lu Yang viciously stomped on the cameraman’s chest cavity, sparks flying everywhere.

"That new character is so crazy and cool, I love it, I’m gonna start a Lu Yang fan club."

"He’s definitely hot, but I’m getting more and more confused about the plot?"

"Guys, we’ve got a big problem, I live nearby the crew, and the roof of their building has been blown off!"

A comment in the viewer section caught the attention of the onlookers.

The comment included a recording of the crew’s building with its roof blown off, and one could faintly hear the sound of police sirens, chaos ensuing.

"Is this... this doesn’t seem like filming?"

"They probably didn’t notify the local police, otherwise how do you explain the new character wiping out all the Mutant Beasts on Sky Sea Star with a single sword strike? That’s definitely special effects."

"I have a feeling that move wasn’t special effects."

"If it’s not special effects, I’ll stand on my head to wash my hair!"

Police helicopters circled the building, countless spotlights converged on Lu Yang, illuminating the crime scene.

Crime scene: crew members were dismembered by Lu Yang, the director was beaten the worst, pleading miserably for Lu Yang to spare him.

A robot policeman kicked down the door, showing off a dense array of gun barrels: "Freeze!"

As if Lu Yang didn’t see the policemen at all, he continued to pack the crew members into Black Canopy Boats, instructing the boats to autonomously take these people to Sky Sea Star, then come back to him.

The police had never seen such a brazen criminal, and immediately ordered to open fire.

Bang bang bang—

Countless bullets were fired at Lu Yang, but as they neared him, they all stopped in midair, frozen.

[ Freeze ]

This was the Rule set by Lu Yang.

"What?!"

The pupils of the remote police operators controlling the robots contracted. What kind of technology was this?!

"Sorry, but my companion said these are bad people, none can be spared. After giving it some thought, it’s best to hand them over to the victims for justice."

Chapter 1287: 1286 "My Significant Other

"What is this method?" The bullet lost its kinetic energy in an instant, completely still, leaving both the police and the audience with their jaws dropped.

"His energy shield must have a limit, it can’t stay on forever, fire, keep firing!"

Immediately, the police chief gave the order, which was not his decision alone, as the quantum computer provided the same calculation result.

According to the quantum computer, there was a ninety-five percent probability that the energy barrier surrounding Lu Yang could not be sustained for long; the more bullets congealed, the shorter the duration.

Right after the police chief’s order, they immediately received a higher order: "Superiors have ordered to capture him alive!"

"What?"

Higher-up leaders noticed Lu Yang’s presence, the technology he possessed, and the forces behind him; these were all things the Interstellar Alliance wanted to know, and he absolutely could not die.

But it was too late, the command was given, the rooftop was ablaze with gunfire, hot like a furnace.

Bang bang bang—

The robotic police were covered with gun muzzles, densely packed like a wasp nest, firing different caliber bullets and laser guns, and some weapons that Lu Yang couldn’t identify.

Among the audience were military enthusiasts who recognized that these were the police’s most powerful weapons available; they could collapse a building, let alone hit a person!

Countless attacks came from all sides.

However, regardless of the type of attack, they all congealed at a distance close to Lu Yang, not more not less, just exactly three feet.

Even the exploding shells stopped three feet away, frozen just as they were bursting.

Neither the high-ranking officials of the Interstellar Alliance nor the ordinary audience could believe their eyes, and this could no longer be explained by an energy shield!

Lu Yang waved at the camera, revealing a mysterious smile, and incomprehensible vocabulary flowed from his lips.

He said, "Trace Origin."

Like time reversing, the fired bullets retreated through the barrels back into the magazines, the exploded shells returned to their original form, the opened rooftop closed up, and Lu Yang himself disappeared without a trace, as if nothing had happened.

If the film crew hadn’t disappeared as well.

"What, what is this technology?" The comment section was in an uproar, with viewers continuously posting question marks, but no one could provide a definite answer.

"Is it the theoretical technology of atomic repair?"

"Time reversal, it must be time reversal!"

Immediately, the Alliance’s high command deployed their most advanced warship to chase the Black Canopy Boat, but after the boat performed one Shrinking Ground Into Inches, they could no longer track its whereabouts.

No one noticed that the target they were most eager to find was right under the building.

Disguised, Lu Yang hummed a tune as he left the site of the incident, his clothes transformed into a common style of the Interstellar Alliance, his appearance changed, even his aura was different from before. Even Old Meng, standing here, would not recognize him.

He wasn’t ready to contact the high-ranking officials of the Interstellar Alliance yet.

"Little Yang, what does ’my other half’ mean?" Fairy Eternity’s cheeks flushed slightly, feeling the intimacy of the term, and she couldn’t even look straight at Little Yang now.

"My home is the Spiritual Space, Fairy, you live in my Spiritual Space, naturally, you are my other half." Lu Yang observed Fairy’s reaction with a nonchalant smile, finding it amusing.

"So that’s what it means?" Fairy Eternity suddenly understood, then her eyes lit up, "Then can I call Little Yang that too?"

"No, no need to address me like that." Lu Yang averted his gaze, unwilling to continue the topic.

...

Naturally, the Interstellar Alliance’s high-ranking officials didn’t know that the alien who stirred their nerves tonight was feeling shy.

Twelve projections surrounded a round table, the atmosphere heavy.

Half of them had been woken up from sleep to attend this meeting.

Since the founding of the Alliance, there have been few incidents that elicited such a response from them, and once they had such reactions, the events encountered were inevitably major events that affected the direction of the Alliance.

The last time there was such an atmosphere, it was about resolving the imminent disintegration of the Alliance.

"Has the Ether’s calculation about that extraterrestrial visitor come up yet?" One of them slowly asked; he was the Chairman of the Interstellar Alliance Council, the highest leader of the Interstellar Alliance.

Ether is the Interstellar Alliance’s most advanced and vast quantum computer; the Interstellar Alliance has eight hundred main stars, three thousand auxiliary stars, and a population estimated in trillions. Maintaining such a vast rule largely credits the comprehensive planning by the quantum computer, Ether.

"It can’t calculate specific results, only probabilistic events; the techniques shown by the extraterrestrial named ’Lu Yang’ have never been recorded in ’Ether’," said the Alliance’s chief scientist, known as the greatest brain of the millennium.

Though now only his brain was his own.

"Either Lu Yang is backed by powers that followed a different research path than ours, or their technological level constitutes a generational gap with ours."

"According to Ether, if their technological level far surpassed ours, their influence would have already spread throughout the universe, and the Alliance would not have only encountered them today."

"So does Ether believe this extraterrestrial possesses technology from a different research path?"

"Yes."

Everyone fell into a long silence, pondering the conclusion given by Ether.

"However, if you insist that the extraterrestrial visitor and the records in Ether correspond, there is actually one ’pseudo-record’ that could match," continued the chief scientist.

The other eleven were stunned: "What do you mean by ’pseudo-record’?"

"It means that there are records, but with no supportive text before and after, and present technology can’t verify those matters, like many civilizations’ enlightenment stages containing great floods that end the world or records of immortals traveling through space."

One of them looked puzzled: "You’re saying it might match, you’re not suggesting that the extraterrestrial visitor is an immortal, are you?"

"Exactly."

This time, no one was silent; they directly rejected the conclusion.

"It’s impossible; we don’t need to consider such a probability."

In their eyes, what immortals? Their technology was also perceived as divine intervention by those lesser civilizations.

"Is the greatest probability that it possesses a different research path from us?"

Everyone showed a hint of joy; the Alliance had experienced a technological bottleneck for five hundred years, not to mention significant breakthroughs, there were hardly a few minor ones. Lu Yang’s mastered techniques could surely help them achieve new progress in science!

"What about the issue with Sky Sea Star?"

"What did Ether suggest?"

"Recommend to initiate humanitarian aid and post-disaster reconstruction for Sky Sea Star. Firstly, this follows the longstanding route of the Alliance; secondly, we can start from Sky Sea Star to learn more about Lu Yang."

"Then follow Ether’s suggestion."

"Also, I propose dedicating one percent of Ether’s computing power to analyze Lu Yang’s situation."

"I agree."

"Agreed."

"Agreed."

Chapter 1288: 1287: A Bustling Library

"Recently, the Interstellar Alliance cracked a major criminal group suspected of crimes against humanity. It is reported that the group, in order to attract attention, disrupted the genetic makeup of alien organisms by disguising a planet as a television program for filming. Currently, the planet has been rescued, and the police are conducting further investigations."

Following that, the Salvation Team, as representatives from Sky Sea Star, visited the Interstellar Alliance and received a warm welcome.

Lu Yang has been at the Interstellar Alliance for five days, and he realized that the streets are filled with promotional posters of the Salvation Team, far more famous than Lu Yang had imagined.

The public, having learned that the "Sky Sea Star Legend" was not a TV series but actual events, became even more enthusiastic about the Salvation Team.

What surprised Lu Yang the most, however, was the emergence of his own fan club.

Although widely revered as a paragon of the Righteous Path while in the Cultivation World, the cultural norm there certainly wouldn’t allow for raising placards and parading through the streets.

The Interstellar Alliance would dare to do so.

Handsome, mysterious, and a champion of justice... These elements combined have made Lu Yang a breakout star of the Alliance.

Whenever Lu Yang thinks about similar events happening on various planets, the corners of his eyes twitch slightly.

While he enjoys the spotlight, the obsession many have with him still scares him a bit.

Discarding his complex thoughts, he walked into the library.

He had been spending his days in the library learning about the history of the Interstellar Alliance.

The history of the Interstellar Alliance, even summarized, would fill an entire row of bookshelves; simply put, it is a melting pot of various planetary civilizations.

Initially, the Interstellar Alliance only consisted of twelve members, but as more life-sustaining planets were discovered, these planets joined the Alliance, eventually forming the massive entity it is today.

However, Lu Yang could not find any records of the Candle Sky Civilization, suggesting that ships from Candle Sky have not reached here yet.

Naturally, there is also nothing about the Blue Star Civilization.

"The technology of the Interstellar Alliance is truly advanced," Lu Yang couldn’t help but remark.

"They’ve built devices to capture solar energy and are engaged in constructing on a stellar scale."

"They manufacture signal simulators for brains; mechanical bodies whose touch sensation can fully transmit to the brain, though this technology is expensive and unaffordable for the common folks."

"The most powerful weapon is the Star Annihilation Cannon, capable of blasting high-energy particles through a Star Core."

"And there are high-speed quantum computers and Ether Computers that can compute anything based on input data."

Immortal Fairy sat on the bed, swaying her little feet left and right, completely oblivious to what Little Yang was impressed by.

The capturing of solar energy is a simple task that the Yaoyang Sect can manage, brain signal simulation is just the Illusionary Realm.

And the Ether Computer that Little Yang kept praising, it sounds rather annoying.

"What’s so great about that? My sharp mind can deduce Dao Fruit, can this Ether Computer do that?"

Immortal Fairy made up her mind; if Little Yang didn’t explain it properly, she’d ignore him for a year... too long... a month... also too long.

Just all day, to annoy him!

Lu Yang hurriedly placated Immortal Fairy, whose discontent was evident on her face: "Of course, of course, such gadgets cannot compare to a Fairy who is naturally clever and can foresee through the ages."

"Hee hee, you have good taste, I forgive you."

Lu Yang shook his head helplessly and continued studying the culture of the Interstellar Alliance.

He pulled out a foldable map from an astronomy book, depicting the universe explored by the Interstellar Alliance.

Immortal Fairy disdainfully scoffed, "They’ve only explored so little, at most a corner of the universe."

"Yingtian Immortal and the others could randomly point out someone’s territory, and it would be larger than this area."

Lu Yang thought about asking how large the Fairy’s territory was, but after careful consideration, for safety’s sake, decided it’s better not to ask this kind of question that might provoke Immortal Fairy to scratch herself.

"The map is already big enough."

Just this map alone is worth the trip, and coupled with so many technological products from the Interstellar Alliance, it could completely rejuvenate the Cultivation World.

A chip the size of a fingernail fell out from between the pages, and Lu Yang picked it up and examined it under the light. Unable to figure out its purpose, he decided to put the chip back where it was.

Just as this thought surfaced, he heard an explosion not far away, followed by alarm sounds and the panicked voices of civilians.

Lu Yang looked in the direction of the sound and saw a rather luxurious sports car crash into the library, a bloodied young man emerging from it.

Then, several more cars crashed into the library, each releasing a squad of fully armed robots, apparently there to capture the young man.

Through the gaping hole created by the crash, the outside world appeared in total chaos, seemingly a result of a high-speed chase between the young man and the robot army.

The young man stumbled and ran, gunfire and bullets raining down on him, making it a very dangerous scene.

However, the young man was fortunate; the bullets only scraped by him without hitting.

People from the Interstellar Alliance are not accustomed to reading physical books, and there were scarcely any people in the library; on seeing the danger, they all fled the scene desperately.

Lu Yang felt it necessary to leave the library along with these people.

The reason was simple: the young man was running towards him.

Lu Yang quickly ducked into a gap between bookshelves.

Then he saw the young man also quickly duck beside him.

The two of them faced each other.

Lu Yang: "!!!"

Not cool, man, why are you coming over here?

"Get down!"

The young man suddenly shouted and hurriedly tackled Lu Yang.

Machine gun bullets swept over their heads, piercing through several rows of bookshelves.

One could imagine that if the young man hadn’t tackled Lu Yang, Lu Yang would still have been unharmed.

While pinning Lu Yang down, the young man rolled and threw a bomb, which exploded and temporarily put the robots into a frozen state, immobilizing them.

The young man turned his head and seriously warned Lu Yang, "Don’t think we are safe now. That was my last pulse bomb, it only temporarily disables them. We must find the chip my grandfather left here to solve this for good!"

Lu Yang thought the young man was so capable, he probably did not need his help, after all, he was just here for a visit.

Seeing Lu Yang’s reluctance to assist, the young man said sternly, "The outside is already surrounded by the robot army, neither of us can escape. Our only option now is to find that chip!"

"Do you want to know why we are being chased? Because my grandfather discovered that Artificial Intelligence wants to topple human rule!"

Lu Yang took out the fingernail-sized chip from the book again and asked, "Is this what you want?"

The young man was overjoyed, took it and compared it for a while, then disappointingly returned it to Lu Yang.

"No, not this one. There should be my grandfather’s mark on the right chip."

Lu Yang was even more confused; where did the chip sandwiched in the book come from?

Just as Lu Yang was puzzled, another explosion sounded from the other side of the library. A valiant young woman also stumbled in, being chased.

The woman had good eyesight; despite the distance and the smoke, she could clearly see what Lu Yang was holding.

Her eyes lit up, and excitedly she said, "That’s the one, the evidence of the financial tycoon’s illegal exploitation of the Constant Star is right here!"

Lu Yang: "..."

Maybe I shouldn’t have come to the library today?

Chapter 1289: 1288: Melee

The girl’s eyes lit up when she saw the chip in Lu Yang’s hands, but her face turned grave upon noticing the robots gradually regaining their mobility.

"The conglomerate not only sent people to hunt me down but also sent robots after me!"

She felt a sense of foreboding, having not anticipated that the conglomerate would be so thorough as to lay an ambush with robots in this location.

No matter what, I must get my hands on that chip first!

The girl, agile and swift, glanced back at the pursuers, pushed over tables and chairs to block their path, and flipped her way to Lu Yang’s side.

She spoke to Lu Yang earnestly, "I don’t know who you are, but the chip in your hands is incredibly dang..."

"Take it."

Without a word, Lu Yang stuffed the chip into the girl’s hand, a decisiveness that left her momentarily stunned.

Realizing it wasn’t the time to be dazed, she quickly checked the chip.

"Strange, why isn’t it this one?"

She had thought that finding a chip the size of a fingernail in the library meant it was the one left by the contact, but after inspecting it, she realized she was mistaken—It was just a similar-looking chip.

She shook her head disappointment and handed the chip back to Lu Yang, "This isn’t the chip I’m looking for."

Lu Yang: "..."

So whose chip is it, then?

"Forget the chip, how do we deal with the enemies on both sides?" the boy asked anxiously, not sure if he was talking to himself or someone else.

Among the three people on the scene, only Lu Yang was not in a hurry. The boy and the girl were as anxious as ants on a hot pan. To their left were the operational robots, and to their right were the conglomerate’s mercenaries. There was nowhere to escape.

But they couldn’t afford to flee, as the chip was nearby. Artificial Intelligence and the conglomerate were already aware of its presence here. If they escaped, the chip would fall into someone else’s hands, putting humanity at risk!

They noticed Lu Yang’s calm demeanor, out of place in the chaotic environment, and sighed internally—it seemed that an innocent bystander dragged into this dangerous situation had already given up on thinking.

The robots and mercenaries didn’t dare to make their move lightly, guns pointed at each other, each mistaking the other as the target’s ally.

For a moment, an uneasy standoff filled the air.

Suddenly, the third wall of the library was smashed open, breaking the standoff. Another girl barged in, golden hair and blue eyes gleaming, her delicate features elfin. Upon seeing the chip in Lu Yang’s hands, her eyes brightened.

"Found it!"

This girl also had pursuers behind her, but unlike before, these were real people.

The new pursuers were shocked to see the robots and mercenaries: "So there are indeed allies!"

Acting first, the new pursuers opened fire, and the robots and mercenaries returned fire, plunging the place into chaos!

Amidst the chaos, the golden-haired, blue-eyed girl dodged bullets and found Lu Yang.

"This chip..."

"Take it."

The golden-haired, blue-eyed girl checked the chip and exhaled in relief, "This is the one!"

Lu Yang also breathed a sigh of relief, happy to have found the rightful owner of the chip.

"So you were indeed the contact person arranged by the doctor!"

She spoke with conviction, having escaped the organization with the doctor’s help. Alas, the doctor took a bullet protecting her and died. The doctor, before passing away, asked her to find the chip in the library, though he couldn’t finish his sentence before drawing his last breath, likely wanting to inform her about the contact in the library.

"Err, miss, you might be mistaken. I’m not any sort of contact person," Lu Yang felt it necessary to clarify.

"You must be. Otherwise, why would you hand over the chip to me the moment we met?" The golden-haired, blue-eyed girl grew even more certain of Lu Yang’s identity. To her, it was a simple intelligence test.

"I really am not."

The golden-haired, blue-eyed girl surprised, "What, wasn’t it the professor who recorded the organization’s illegal genetic modifications on this chip? And then he entrusted it to you to rendezvous with me—a genetic modifi...

Lu Yang wanted to prevent the girl from spilling the beans, but she spoke as fast as a machine gun, explaining the whole situation in a few sentences.

"I came here to read books," Lu Yang said, helpless, holding up the book about the cosmic map.

Seeing Lu Yang persistently denying, the golden-haired, blue-eyed girl was convinced she had thought too much and quickly apologized, "Oh, I’m sorry, it was a case of mistaken identity. I’ve dragged you into this disaster!"

"What, it’s this book!" The boy noticed the book in Lu Yang’s hands, and his body shook with excitement, an idea forming.

"Yes, how could I not think of it? My grandfather’s favorite book was this one; he said that the universe was infinite, and compared to it, the Interstellar Alliance was but a drop in the ocean, completely insignificant. The Interstellar Alliance should hold the universe in awe."

The boy, hugging his grandfather, learned about the mysteries of the universe on the rooftop during his childhood. Just thinking of those moments from his youth made him break down in tears.

Only his grandfather and he knew about this; only he could find the chip his grandfather left behind!

The boy took the cosmic map from the book, pointed to a particular Star Domain, and murmured to himself while turning the pages among the astronomy section’s shelves: "According to the layout, it should be right here."

The first girl noticed the book’s author in Lu Yang’s hands, and her body shook.

"Why didn’t I think of it? The author of this book is an alias used by our resistance organization’s Boss."

The first girl took out a mysterious slip from her pocket, which she had received before coming to the library but never knew its purpose.

Now she understood—the numbers on the mysterious slip corresponded to the pages and words in this book!

She took Lu Yang’s book and hurriedly translated the enigmatic slip.

At that moment, the shooting continued among the three forces, with gunshots exploding near their ears, like thunder. The boy and the girl raced against time among the bookshelves, searching for their chip.

"Found it!"

Both the boy and the girl found their chips and couldn’t help but rejoice, but the sound of gunfire soon snapped them back to reality.

The boy proudly showed off the chip he held, "My grandfather’s chip contains a virus that can paralyze robots and mechanical bodies. We’ll just make our run for it when it’s time!"

The conglomerate’s mercenaries were mostly mechanical.

The golden-haired, blue-eyed girl reacted with shock, "No, that won’t work, those hunting me are genetically modified humans; the virus won’t affect them!"

The first girl pulled out a bomb, "I have a tear gas grenade. We can catch those genetically modified humans off guard with it!"

"Great idea!"

The boy peeled back the skin on his arm, revealing a mechanical arm underneath. He inserted the chip into the mechanical arm and tapped a few times on the screen. The sound of combat suddenly died down—it was the robots and mercenaries stricken by the virus, taking hits helplessly.

The first girl threw the tear gas grenade, which exploded with a bang, flooding the area with choking fumes and blinding the genetically modified humans.

The three young men and women seized the opportunity to escape, weaving through the battling forces in a risky and exhilarating escape, not forgetting to pull along the innocent bystander, Lu Yang.

Chapter 1290: 1289: Everyone Has a Tragic Past

"Alas, we really escaped!"

The young boys and girls, having survived a disaster, congratulated each other with high-fives, all but Lu Yang, who remained unresponsive throughout.

"My name is Lan Hai." The boy introduced himself proactively. Although they had just met, the life-or-death situation made them feel like long-time friends.

"My name is Yu Ran." The first girl introduced herself.

"My name is Mi Xue’er." This was the blonde, blue-eyed girl.

The three of them turned to look at Lu Yang, who had been silent the whole time, their gazes filled with an unspoken expectation.

Since others had introduced themselves, out of politeness, Lu Yang felt obliged to do the same: "My name is Meng Jingzhou."

"So you are Brother Meng, what an unwarranted disaster today!" Lan Hai sighed with empathy for Lu Yang.

Lu Yang also felt it was indeed an unwarranted disaster.

"Actually, I didn’t expect things to develop this far. Originally, I was just sorting through the things my grandfather left behind..."

Lu Yang quickly interrupted Lan Hai’s reminiscing: "Stop, don’t say anymore, I’m just a bystander, I don’t want to know so much."

Lan Hai shook his head: "No, Brother Meng, face the reality, you’re not just a bystander anymore. Although it was me who got the chip, ’Ether’ saw both you and me acquiring it together, it will definitely not let you go."

Lu Yang: "..."

Are you guys from the Interstellar Alliance always this hostile to tourists?

"But Brother Meng, you’re right, it’s not the time to talk, let’s get in the car and we can talk."

Lan Hai opened the sports car, the one that had crashed into the library. The car had smashed down the library without even scratching its paint, attesting to its terrifying quality.

Lu Yang had no choice but to resign himself to getting in the car and listening to Lan Hai’s stories.

Of course, there was another reason to get into the car – Fairy Eternity wanted to hear the story.

The two girls also got into the car.

"My grandfather is Lan Kang, although not well-known in the Alliance, but he is definitely the most formidable scientist in the entire Alliance. He is also the mentor of the Alliance’s chief scientist, Gao Huanyu."

"This sports car was modified by my grandfather."

"A month ago, my grandfather suddenly passed away. At first, I didn’t suspect anything, until I was sorting through his belongings and found his notebook, which contained notes about the awakening of Artificial Intelligence, and Ether’s plans to rebel."

"To stop Ether’s rebellion, we had to get the chip from the library, and hand it to the Chief Scientist, Grandpa Gao Huanyu, so he could use it to format Ether."

"However, Ether became aware of this, and I couldn’t contact Grandpa Gao through any technological means; I could only meet him in person."

"That’s why I came to the library to look for the chip."

"So your chip is that important," Yu Ran hadn’t realized Lan Hai had such a complicated past.

"I come from an organization called ’Star Group’, founded by a scientist employed by the Murong Family."

"The Murong Family is responsible for the mining of the Constellation Stars above our heads. The boss accidentally discovered that the Murong Family was conducting illegal and violent mining of the Constellation Stars."

"Such actions could indeed bring huge benefits to the Murong Family, but they also have the potential to cause the Constellation Stars to disintegrate and explode, and once a star explodes, the death toll would be calculated in hundreds of millions!"

"The boss tried to persuade the Murong Family Patriarch to stop this behavior, but the patriarch, blinded by greed, did not heed the warning."

"The boss secretly established the Star Group, gathered evidence against the Murong Family, and planned to report them to the Federation Supreme Council."

"Our undercover agents successfully embedded within the Murong financial empire have collected the evidence and concealed it in the astronomical section of the library. I was responsible for retrieving the evidence for the Star Group, but somehow my whereabouts were exposed, and the Murong Family’s mercenaries have been chasing me to the library!"

"Fortunately, I got the evidence. Now all I need to do is bring the chip back to the Star Group, and my mission will be accomplished!"

Just as Yu Ran was feeling relieved that the task was completed without any mishap, she suddenly received a voice message.

She opened the voice message and amidst intense gunfire, a deep male voice said: "Yu Ran, do not come back, the Murong Family has also planted an undercover among us, that’s how your whereabouts were exposed. The Murong Family’s people have already started to come after you, protect the chip, do not come back!"

Yu Ran shuddered, unexpected changes occurred, and tears flowed down in big drops.

Seeing the grim atmosphere in the vehicle, Mi Xue’er decided to share her story to divert Yu Ran’s attention.

"I am an orphan who was adopted by the Supreme Council as a child. They called it adoption, but in reality, they used me as a test subject."

"The Supreme Council believes that the human body is the most powerful, they reject mechanical modifications, and to make the human body stronger, they constantly performed experiments on me."

"Eventually, the professor regarded me as his granddaughter, and unable to bear seeing me continue to undergo experiments and modifications, he ran away with me against the will of the Supreme Council. He was hit by a stray bullet while protecting me, and before dying, he told me there was a chip hidden in the library that could overthrow the Supreme Council, and directed me to find the chip."

"The next generation of Genetic Modifiers from the Supreme Council were born, and even the robot army couldn’t match up. The Supreme Council is about to start a rebellion, we must stop them."

Fairy Eternity, after listening, rolled around in bed, murmuring softly: "Why is this Interstellar Alliance so dangerous? It’s either robot rebellions or exploding stars."

Minister echoed on the side: "Yeah, looking at how prosperous this Alliance seems, who would have thought it hides so many crises."

After Mi Xue’er finished speaking, the atmosphere in the car became even more somber, Lan Hai’s grandfather died, Yu Ran’s Star Group was gone, and Mi Xue’er’s professor grandfather also died.

Lu Yang felt that these three people were simply walking disasters; wherever they went, trouble followed. If he had known about their backgrounds earlier, he would not have gotten involved in this mess.

After all three stories were shared, they all glanced at Lu Yang, hoping he would say something to lighten the mood.

Lu Yang sighed, then shared his own experiences.

"I was an orphan with no parents, later Eldest Senior Sister took me in. Like the Supreme Council, Eldest Senior Sister believes that the human body is the most powerful, and she wanted to train me in that direction."

"For this, she made me carry water tanks every day, continuing until both my arms completely lost sensation before it could end. At night, she would scald me with boiling water, making me jump and hop around."

"Later, a female voice appeared in my mind, constantly telling me mythical stories. Sometimes she would take over my body and make me do things against my will."

"I also have a good brother, young and wealthy, also handsome, but with a peculiar personality, not interested in women. I saw that this was not good and advised him to find a girlfriend, but he got angry with me."

"People around me also formed a resistance organization, aiming to overthrow the local regime. Although our organization was small, just over a dozen people, some of them weren’t human, they were animals."

The looks they gave Lu Yang carried pity, all suffering similar fates: an abusive Senior Sister, a possibly split-personality Lu Yang, a friend with unusual sexual orientation, and a sounding very feeble organization.

Chapter 1291: 1290: Brother Meng ought to be the weakest among the four of us.

After a frank exchange, the four of them discovered they each had tragic pasts and, being in the same boat, were currently pursued by three different forces, bringing them significantly closer to each other.

"What do you guys plan to do next?" Lu Yang asked casually, as he didn’t really care and was just along for the ride to see the world with the other three.

"My data chip contains evidence of Murong Group’s illegal activities. As long as it’s released online, it will draw public attention, and the Supreme Council will sanction the Murong Group," Yu Ran said. The means to contact the Supreme Council were only available to their Boss, but now that the Star Group had been wiped out by the Murong Group and the Boss was MIA, their only option was to go public with the evidence.

Lan Hai shook his head, "It won’t work. Ether controls all the networks. Because I’m with you now, it will identify you as my accomplices. You won’t be able to send out any messages."

"I’m afraid Ether knows exactly where we are now."

Yu Ran was disheartened. She had obtained critical evidence of the crimes but was unable to stop the Murong Group. However, there was something she didn’t understand, "Then why hasn’t Ether continued to send robot troops to hunt us down?"

Letting go of the steering wheel and allowing the car to drive itself, Lan Hai explained to Yu Ran. The car was offline, so there was no worry about Ether taking control. "The previous pursuit with robots targeting me could be explained away as a programming error, fooling the people of the Alliance. But if robots were sent one after another to chase us, the Alliance would start to suspect a bigger issue. Right now, Ether isn’t willing to expose itself."

"What are your plans, Mi Xue’er?" Lu Yang then asked.

Mi Xue’er was equally downcast, "Originally, I also planned to make the evidence public, but according to Lan Hai, that won’t work either."

"But I have another plan."

"What plan?"

"The professor included a formula for ’Anti-Gene Agent’ in the chip. The Anti-Gene Agent can incapacitate us, gene modified folks. If I have the formula and access to a lab, I can create the Anti-Gene Agent, and we won’t have to fear the Supreme Council’s pursuit!" Mi Xue’er confidently declared. She was not just skilled in combat, but also quite an expert in gene technology.

"A lab, huh..." Lan Hai muttered to himself, then had a sudden inspiration, "I’ve got it; I have a good buddy who loves to experiment. He’s got a small lab at home. We can go there!"

"That’s great!"

With a plan in place, Lan Hai changed the destination to his friend’s house. The sports car drifted around a corner, leaving behind a trail of cool tail lights.

Lan Hai’s sports car was incredibly fast, covering from one end of the city to the other in about an hour.

"Stop the car," Lan Hai suddenly said, puzzling the others.

"What’s wrong?"

Lan Hai pointed to a roadside toilet sign: "I need to use the restroom. Do you guys need to go?"

Yu Ran shook her head, "No need. Most of my body has been mechanically modified; I don’t need to use the restroom."

Mi Xue’er also shook her head, "My body has been genetically modified so that I virtually don’t need food or water."

Lan Hai scratched his head, thinking how each of his two beautiful companions was less and less like a normal person, while he had only modified both arms.

"I’ll go down too," Lu Yang spoke up. He actually noticed a small convenience store by the road and wanted to buy some snacks.

"Do you guys have any cash? Lend me some?"

"Should have some." Lan Hai rummaged through several pockets and pulled out a couple of banknotes.

"Let’s not talk about lending amongst ourselves, it’s too formal, don’t pay it back," Lan Hai generously said.

"Thanks."

Lu Yang and Lan Hai got out of the car, leaving just Yu Ran and Mi Xue’er behind.

"Yu Ran, is your modified body more geared towards combat?" Mi Xue’er suddenly asked.

Yu Ran was taken aback, "How do you know?"

She’s had a love for combat since a young age, so even after mechanical modifications, it was all geared towards combat.

But she had never revealed her modification model, so how did Mi Xue’er figure it out?

Mi Xue’er smiled, pointing to herself, "I can’t explain the principle, perhaps it’s a result of genetic modification. I can roughly sense the threat level of people nearby. Among the three of you, Yu Ran, you give off the highest sense of danger."

"That’s amazing?" Yu Ran was surprised. The Supreme Council really had some substances.

"What about Lan Hai and Brother Meng?"

"I don’t feel any threat from them; both are ordinary people. If I really had to compare, Lan Hai is somewhat stronger than Brother Meng."

...

"Do you have any best-selling snacks here? I’d like to buy some."

The shopkeeper is a robot, and its face screen displays several types of snacks: "I recommend these."

"I’ll take one of each."

"Alright."

Lu Yang, carrying a large bag of Spirit Stones, picked out a lollipop-like thing and put it in his mouth, the coolness refreshing.

"Tastes good, Fairy Eternity, you should try it." Lu Yang switched bodies; he wasn’t that fond of sweets, he mainly wanted to let Fairy Eternity have a taste.

"Not bad at all."

Fairy Eternity’s response surprised Lu Yang, as he thought she was going to comment that the lollipop wasn’t tasty compared to the food she made.

"As long as Fairy Eternity likes it."

On the way back, a menacing man in a black coat and black hat blocked Lu Yang’s path.

The menacing man threw a small device, enveloping him and Lu Yang.

"Kid, looks like you’re Mi Xue’er’s new companion."

"Who are you?"

The menacing man lifted the black hat, revealing a fierce face with a cross-shaped scar, intimidating to behold.

Then, one by one, more than thirty Genetic modifiers appeared, each exuding a powerful and menacing aura, holding all sorts of bizarre weapons.

"Hehe, kid, just blame it on being Mi Xue’er’s friend. If you honestly tell us the identities and abilities of the people in the car, I might kindly grant you a quick death!" another Genetic modifier sneered, his power seems related to ice, as the temperature around him dropped significantly.

"Don’t even think about screaming for help, see this small device on the ground? It can simulate the surrounding environment, making it look like nothing’s wrong from the outside, and your voice naturally cannot be heard!"

Lu Yang trembled with fear, guessing their intention: "Do you want to get information from me and then ambush Mi Xue’er and the others!"

"I will not betray my companions!"

"It seems Mi Xue’er did find herself a good companion, then go to your death!"

Five minutes later.

Lu Yang, holding several bags, got into the car and said: "Let’s go."

"Oh, Lan Hai, here’s your money back."

Lan Hai took the money, puzzled: "Didn’t you spend any?"

"No, I just met a kind person on the way who gave me some money."

Lan Hai gave it no further thought and continued to drive the sports car.

However, as soon as the car started, a skeletal man appeared in the middle of the road, his arms long, swinging at the car.

The sports car braked urgently, avoiding the strike, leaving a deep trench on the road.

"Mi Xue’er, aren’t you ready to come out?" the skeletal man laughed wildly.

Mi Xue’er and the others knew that staying in the car meant certain death, so they had no choice but to get out.

Mi Xue’er, ready for a fierce battle, warned Lu Yang and the other two: "Be careful, this man is a Class A Warrior of the Supreme Council—Harvester; I’ll hold him, you take the chance to escape!"

Harvester laughed loudly: "Mi Xue’er, do you think you can still run? Let me tell you, the organization has dispatched more than thirty warriors to surround you completely!"

"Here we have Class A Warriors ’Extreme Cold Freeze,’ ’Fierce Sun,’ ’Life Cycler,’ ’Ghost Face,’ ’Seven Blades,’ and the rest are Class B Warriors!"

Harvester spread his arms wide and shouted: "Come out!"

No one responded.

Chapter 1292: 1291: Ah, I’ve Been Kidnapped

"Where are the people?" Harvester’s heart pounded with anxiety; this shouldn’t be happening.

They said they were doing something unimportant before, just went to the bathroom.

And after coming back, according to the plan, they were to intercept the vehicle. How come all his teammates were gone?

Did they retreat?

If they retreated, they should have signaled him.

"Come out, everyone!" Harvester tried once more to summon his companions, but still no one responded.

Harvester cursed inwardly, calling them all unreliable bastards. Since things have come to this, he had no choice but to act on his own!

Lu Yang was also surprised by Harvester’s appearance. After taking care of those Genetically Modified Soldiers, he had found a hideout belonging to the Supreme Council nearby, following what those people had said.

Thinking it was a matter of convenience, he went there and used some tricks, making everyone from the hideout surrender themselves to the police.

He had taken care of everything so thoroughly, and yet Harvester, this one that slipped through the net, remained.

Indeed, haste makes waste.

"You all go first!" Mi Xue’er pushed everyone away and faced Harvester.

"I’ll help too!" Yu Ran volunteered.

Harvester had just clashed with Mi Xue’er when he realized something was wrong: "You’ve been hiding your true strength!"

The information they had on Mi Xue’er didn’t indicate she was this strong!

Yu Ran’s attacks followed one after another. She might not be as strong as Mi Xue’er or Harvester, but she was enough to create opportunities for Mi Xue’er to attack.

Outmatched by the two women, Harvester thought of retreating, but escaping wasn’t so simple at this point.

He feigned a flaw, Mi Xue’er with her lack of combat experience indeed fell for it, and he seized the chance to rush at Lu Yang, grabbing his neck. His hand, his most lethal weapon, could sever Lu Yang’s neck with just a bit more force!

"Don’t move, or I’ll kill him!"

Mi Xue’er and Yu Ran, who were about to rush over, abruptly halted, not daring to make another move.

"Ah, I’m being held hostage," said Lu Yang, surrendering with both hands raised.

With a sneer, Harvester held Lu Yang while walking backwards. Before leaving, he threw a piece of paper.

"If you want this person to live, go to this place!"

The Supreme Council had another hideout nearby, and he wanted to lure Mi Xue’er and the others into it!

Mi Xue’er picked up the note with a grim expression and stood up, but by then, Harvester and Lu Yang were nowhere to be seen.

...

Harvester ran with Lu Yang, trying to contact his companions, still with no response. A bad feeling welled up inside him.

He entered the underground secret facility using the elevator.

The secret facility was brightly lit, yet empty. A cup of coffee on the table was still steaming.

Moreover, there were no signs of a struggle, causing Harvester to shudder with horror. It was as if, due to some emergency, all the branch members had evacuated.

"Hey, Extreme Cold Freeze, are you here—"

"Ether, if you’re here, just give me a sign—"

Harvester’s voice echoed down the long corridor, but there was no reply.

Suddenly a voice came from the end of the corridor: "Mr. Harvester, I am here."

Harvester was overjoyed and hurried over. I knew someone would be here.

However, what stood at the end of the corridor was a Housekeeping Robot. The robot looked at Harvester with its empty gaze, the emergency lights flickering on and off behind it: "Mr. Harvester, do you need some coffee?"

Seeing this scene, Harvester was so frightened he kept retreating step by step.

Gasping for breath heavily, as if he had just been through an invisible fight for life and death, he went to the surveillance room to check the footage after his departure.

The screen showed everything was normal after he left. Harvester, frustrated and perplexed, commanded the footage to fast forward.

The branch that was originally filled with people disappeared in the blink of an eye, scaring the Harvester into a shudder.

He shivered and commanded to rewind and play back the footage, slow it to two times, four times... up to thirty-two times slower, but he still couldn’t see clearly what had happened.

In the slowed-down footage, the people in the branch disappeared one by one. They did not seem to notice their companions’ disappearances until they themselves vanished, as if some indescribable entity silently devoured everyone.

Thinking of his teammates who had vanished, extreme panic spread in his heart. The air circulation system in the branch was functioning normally, the fluorescent lights were warm, but the Harvester felt like the branch was the mouth of a monster, dark and gaping, waiting for him to fall into the trap.

Someone patted him on the shoulder, startling him into jumping: "Are you alright?"

The Harvester turned around and heaved a sigh of relief; it was Lu Yang, he wasn’t the only one here after all.

And Lu Yang’s expressionless face also gave him a bit of confidence.

"There are only two of us here, aren’t you afraid?"

"Two people?" Lu Yang showed a hint of confusion, "Haven’t there always been three of us here?"

As soon as he finished speaking, a yellow-clad figure slowly appeared in the air.

"Ah—"

The Harvester completely broke down, fear totally engulfing his heart.

Ding dong—

The sound of the elevator rang out, and like seeing a savior, the Harvester ran frantically towards it.

It was the Blueprint trio who had taken the elevator to rescue Lu Yang.

The Harvester, having long forgotten the mission, ran madly towards the Blueprint trio, giving them quite a fright.

"Freeze, don’t move!" Mi Xue’er stood at the forefront, warily eyeing the Harvester.

"Run, there are ghosts, there are ghosts!" the Harvester shouted frantically, uttering incomprehensible words.

Mi Xue’er frowned, subdued the Harvester with ease, and warily surveyed her surroundings, also finding it odd that there was no one in the branch.

She had been ready to fight tooth and nail.

Could it be a trap?

"Mi Xue’er, Yu Ran, Blueprint, I’m over here." Lu Yang walked out of the surveillance room, greeting his comrades from a distance.

Seeing the calm Lu Yang, the Harvester, frightened, sat on the ground, pointing at Lu Yang and scrabbling backward with his feet.

"Brother Meng, you’re okay, that’s really great." Mi Xue’er and the others, seeing Lu Yang unharmed, breathed a sigh of relief.

"I’m fine; it’s just that after this Harvester brought me here, he picked up someone else’s coffee, didn’t drink it, and then started shouting and getting overly excited when he heard the Housekeeping Robot respond. When the Housekeeping Robot asked him if he wanted coffee, he got so scared."

"I saw he was acting insanely so I took the chance to escape from him." Lu Yang patted his chest, still shaken.

"How strange."

"Yes, really strange, could he be possessed?"

"He kept muttering about seeing a ghost, could he have really seen one?"

"We should be scientific; where in the world are there ghosts, Brother Meng, right?"

Lu Yang nodded, expressing his agreement: "Exactly, there are no ghosts."

"There really are ghosts!" To get Blueprint and the others on his side, the Harvester bravely led them to the surveillance room to see the footage.

However, the footage was just static, nothing could be seen.

"It was you! You deleted it!" the Harvester yelled at Lu Yang, now seeing him as someone possessed by an evil spirit.

Lu Yang shrugged, very innocently: "I didn’t do anything."

Lu Yang was telling the truth; he indeed hadn’t touched the surveillance, and whether it was deleted or not didn’t matter since the Blueprint group couldn’t see him from the footage anyway.

If it wasn’t him, then did Ether delete it?

Does that mean Ether has taken notice of him?

Chapter 1293: 1292: Grandfather with a Doctorate

Blueprint played with the surveillance footage for a long time, trying to restore it, but never succeeded.
