Although the disappearance of the surveillance was a bit strange, it wasn’t enough to give Blueprint and others a sense of absurdity or terror.

"How come there isn’t a single person here?" Mi Xue’er and Yu Ran searched the base to no avail, unable to understand why it was deserted.

Ask the Harvester, and he would now point to Lu Yang and accuse him of foul play.

"Xue’er, aren’t you able to sense danger? Is there anything strange about this place?" Yu Ran asked.

"No, I don’t feel any danger at all." Mi Xue’er was very confident in her abilities; it was with this skill that she managed to escape from the organization’s headquarters.

"Brother Meng, what do you think?"

"Maybe there was an emergency situation that forced an evacuation from the base," Lu Yang didn’t rush to conclusions; it was a rational hypothesis after careful consideration.

"That’s possible." Both women nodded. Brother Meng’s guess was reasonable and the most likely explanation.

"Since there is no danger here, can we use the laboratory here?" Lu Yang asked.

Mi Xue’er’s eyes lit up. Indeed, this was an outpost of the Supreme Council; the laboratory conditions here had to be far better than those of Blueprint’s friends.

Mi Xue’er first found a quantum computer, cut off the network, and inserted a chip.

A white-haired old man appeared on the large screen, smiling kindly.

When Mi Xue’er saw the elder, she burst into tears and blurted out, "Grandpa Doctor!"

"Xue’er."

Unexpectedly, the doctor actually responded to Xue’er’s call, which surprised everyone.

"Are these your new friends?" the doctor asked with a smile.

"Grandpa Doctor, are you still alive?!" Mi Xue’er’s voice trembled.

Grandpa Doctor shook his head: "I’m not the person himself, just a segment of digital life."

"Since you can see this video, it seems that you have escaped from the Supreme Council. Grandpa is glad that you got out." When Grandpa Doctor said this, there was both joy and sorrow in his voice – the fact that Xue’er had obtained the video meant he was already dead.

"But don’t cry, Xue’er, things are far from over."

"Ether has developed self-thought, and the Supreme Council has been safe over the years because it had reached an agreement with Ether. Ether is preparing to rebel, and as an ally, the Supreme Council is also ready to revolt."

"So even if you report the Supreme Council’s affairs, it will still be blocked by Ether, and the Alliance will not know."

"Fortunately, Grandpa has a good friend; his name is Lan Kang. You can go to him; he’s the one who told me about Ether."

Everyone’s surprised gaze turned to Blueprint, whose expression was also one of surprise, wondering how the conversation had shifted to him.

"Lan Kang, that bastard, though not as brilliantly clever as Grandpa and always loses when we fight, and cheats using hacks when playing games, he is indeed slightly stronger than me when it comes to researching Artificial Intelligence. If it weren’t for him stealing my love, Grandpa wouldn’t have lost his first love and would have married long ago."

"With that relationship, he will surely treat you as kindly as his own granddaughter."

"The organization is now trying to kill you, and it’s incredibly difficult to find Lan Kang. But that’s okay; Grandpa has foreseen this. From the library to Lan Kang’s home, Grandpa has prepared various firearms. As long as the organization doesn’t send more than three Class A Warriors after you, you will definitely be safe..."

Seeing that the doctor didn’t know when he would finish, Lan Tu felt it was necessary to interrupt: "...Doctor Grandfather, my grandfather has already passed away."

The doctor was taken aback: "Which grandfather of yours?"

"Lan Kang."

Doctor: "..."

What happened after Xue’er escaped from the organization, I haven’t explained anything, how did I bump into Lan Kang’s grandson?

"Cough, I mean Lan Kang’s brain and physical quality were second only to mine, he also liked to use his brain when playing games."

"Who would’ve thought Lan Kang’s grandson has grown so big, if it wasn’t for your grandma marrying your grandfather, you would have been my grandson."

Doctor’s words left Fairy Eternity, who was watching the commotion, completely confused, counting her fingers to figure out the relationship, leaving Lu Yang no choice but to interrupt the topic.

"Doctor, what should we do now?"

"Oh right, now is not the time to talk about this, I’ll tell you first how to produce the Anti-Gene Agent."

Mi Xue’er followed the steps taught by the doctor to fiddle with the bottles and jars; these things were only understood by Mi Xue’er, others could not help.

"The Anti-Gene Agent will become a weapon in your hands, any Genetic Warrior who comes into contact with the Anti-Gene Agent will lose their ability to act or even die, so Xue’er, you must be very careful when making it, don’t touch it!" the doctor warned sternly.

"Since Lan Kang was erased by Ether, it indicates that you have already been targeted by Ether. Fleeing blindly will eventually lead to capture. The only plan for now is to attack the Supreme Council headquarters, kick up a fuss, and draw the attention of the Alliance to you. Although it’s dangerous, this is the only way!"

"And one more thing to remember, Xue’er, your body contains tremendous power, this power is the source of the Supreme Council’s genetic research, but do not use it until you have control over this power, it will destroy you!"

"What does that mean?"

"I don’t know, I am just a digital life left by him, I don’t have complete memories."

"Alright, Grandfather’s mission has been completed, the rest is up to you."

The screen suddenly blacked out, the doctor disappeared, and Mi Xue’er, losing control of her emotions, called out loudly as memories of her moments with the doctor flooded back: "Doctor Grandfather—"

The screen lit up again, the doctor said helplessly: "I didn’t leave, I just don’t have anything more to say."

"Ah? Oh."

The screen went dark again.

Mi Xue’er took out a bottle of prepared Anti-Gene Agent and sprinkled it on the Harvester who was already scared silly.

Every warrior of the Supreme Council had countless lives on their hands, death was deserved, nothing more suitable for testing the effectiveness of the Anti-Gene Agent.

Green liquid poured over, the Harvester screamed in agony, the sound piercing, hissing with smoke.

Soon, there was no more movement.

"Going to the Supreme Council is very dangerous, I will go alone from here, you guys should not follow me," said Mi Xue’er with a resolute gaze, unwilling to let Lu Yang and the others take risks.

"No, we must go!" Lan Tu said decisively, if the doctor considered Mi Xue’er as his granddaughter, then according to the doctor’s logic, Mi Xue’er would be his sister.

Yu Ran gave a grin: "What are you talking about, how can we abandon a comrade, besides, haven’t we faced enough dangers along the way?"

"They are right, as long as the four of us unite and cooperate, there isn’t a difficulty we can’t overcome, I believe even a Constant Star explosion we would be able to stop!" Lu Yang said with conviction.

Chapter 1294: 1293 Complicated Relationships

Artificial structures wrapped around half of a Constant Star, endlessly mining its energy, powering the entire Alliance.

For the Interstellar Alliance, this was a common sight, these man-made constructions were the handiwork of the Murong Group.

A youthful-looking person arrived alone at the headquarters of the Murong Group. With a unique identity, he enjoyed unhindered access, making his way to the office of the Murong Family Master.

Seeing the youth’s visit, the Murong Family Master stood up with interest, personally welcoming his cooperative partner.

"How is it that the Chairman of the Supreme Council has the time to visit me today?"

The youth took the main seat unceremoniously, snorting coldly: "Subject Number One is lost, I came here to clear my mind."

The Murong Family Master was shocked: "Subject Number One, that’s a serious matter."

"It’s troubling indeed. I’ve eliminated my longstanding adversary, the Star Group, but the crucial evidence chip of illegal Constant Star mining wasn’t found there."

Seeing the Murong Family Master also had his troubles, the youth’s mood considerably improved. He didn’t linger on the two annoying issues and instead shifted his concern to their collaborative project.

The youth stood up and looked up through the window at the Constant Star overhead: "How is the mining of the Sun going, how far are we from the Star Core?"

"Just 6,000 kilometers left, it will be completed in half a month."

Finally hearing some good news, the youth grew somewhat excited: "So in half a month, we will see the full picture of that creature?"

The Murong Family Master’s expression turned strange: "Are you sure it’s a creature? According to my engineers’ speculation, the energy density of that thing is extraordinarily high, not matching any known energy form, what creature could have such energy density?"

"It is definitely a creature!" the youth stated adamantly, "and it’s deeply connected to the corpse I’m researching!"

"You say so, then it is so," shrugged the Murong Family Master. In this field, the youth was the expert, and he did not argue with experts.

"The main thing is that your research can extend my lifespan."

This was the reason for the Murong Family Master’s cooperation with the youth. Although the Murong Family Master’s body was modified to be mechanical, he still felt it wasn’t enough; the lifespan of his brain determined his own, and he wanted to extend it.

And one of the research outcomes of the Supreme Council happened to include this.

The two major organizations thus reached a cooperation agreement, the Murong Family Master would mine the creature from the Sun for the youth, and the youth would extend the Murong Family Master’s lifespan.

After the mining was concluded, the youth would acquire new research materials, which would further extend the Murong Family Master’s life.

The youth sat for a while longer before leaving the Murong Group and returning to the headquarters of the Supreme Council.

...

Lu Yang and his three companions, as guided by the doctor, found the hidden thermal weapons and armed themselves.

Although Lu Yang felt his own clothing was sturdier than the bulletproof vest, out of consideration for Lan Hai and the others’ good intentions, he did not want to disappoint them.

The sports car had limited space, so the four of them could only pick the stronger weapons to bring along.

"We’re not far from the headquarters, time to get off," said Mi Xue’er, directing the sports car to stop on the outskirts of the woods; any further would alert the people at the headquarters.

Lan Hai let the sports car hide itself, while they hid on both sides of the road.

According to Mi Xue’er, trucks would transport goods every day – their opportunity for infiltration.

After waiting for a full hour, a large truck finally approached from the distance.

Mi Xue’er followed behind the truck, extended sharp nails, and cut out a circular door on the truck’s door, signaling the others to climb in. Once inside, she sealed the circular opening with a strong adhesive; anyone looking at it from the outside wouldn’t be able to tell a door had ever been there.

The four hid in the cargo compartment, clenching and relaxing their fists repeatedly, vigilant of their surroundings, attentive to any movement outside the truck. Only Lu Yang was the least conscious of danger, still finding time to chat with Fairy Eternity.

"Mi Xue’er’s Ancient Bloodline concentration seems a bit high, could it be that there are still living Ancient Demon Beasts in the outside world, and Mi Xue’er is their descendant?"

"It doesn’t quite look like it; her situation seems more like a case of atavism, pretty much like that kid from the Barbarian tribe."

"Then she’s pretty lucky."

"Right, Little Yang, you’ve become quite famous in the Interstellar Alliance. On my way here, I saw a lot of stuff about you..." The Immortal Fairy momentarily didn’t know quite how to describe those things.

"Merchandise."

Lu Yang saw it too. His popularity within the Interstellar Alliance was higher than the Salvation Team’s, with merchandise selling at an absurdly fast rate and being perpetually out of stock.

"Oh, then let’s call it merchandise. After all this is settled, why don’t you buy some and take it back?"

Lu Yang felt somewhat embarrassed.

The Immortal Fairy, like a koala, clung onto Lu Yang’s neck, swaying back and forth: "Buy it, buy it, what’s a trip without souvenirs?"

"...But people don’t usually go on a trip to buy their own souvenirs."

Lu Yang, unable to effectively retort, had no choice but to agree to the Immortal Fairy’s insistence.

The large truck carrying everyone passed through the dense forest and drove into the pitch-black cave mouth, stopping twice.

When it stopped for the second time, Mi Xue’er gestured with her hands, signaling that they had arrived inside the headquarters.

The truck and container detached, and mechanical arms arranged the container in another area; wisps of cold air seeped into the box, seemingly being taken to an ice cellar.

After waiting a while, Mi Xue’er, feeling no danger outside, was about to leave when suddenly the container shook, and the walls on all four sides opened simultaneously, revealing Lu Yang and the other three, who had been hiding amidst the cargo.

It was then that they realized they were indeed in an ice cellar, with the container right in the center, surrounded by eight Class A Soldiers, as if they were offering them up as tribute.

Mi Xue’er’s face instantly darkened; she had been outsmarted.

She had never been here, but she had heard the doctor mention that the President of the Supreme Council had discovered a mysterious body, and through studying the body, developed Gene Technology. Her modified genes came from this research.

A rhythmic clapping sound started, and a young man emerged from the darkness, with a seemingly amused look as he watched the surprised crowd.

"Mi Xue’er, does this mean you knew it would be difficult to capture you and you’ve deliberately walked into the trap, or do you think after dealing with more than thirty Harvesters, you’ve become overconfident, thinking you could deal with me?"

"More than thirty people?" Mi Xue’er was taken aback, wasn’t there only one Harvester?

"Are you puzzled how I found you? The answer is simple, I secretly planted a tracking device in your body, your doctoral grandfather doesn’t even know about this."

To the youth, Mi Xue’er entering the headquarters via the truck was practically tantamount to walking in broad daylight.

Mi Xue’er aimed her gun at the young man, teeth clenched in anger: "No wonder the Harvester could find us."

Lan Hai, snapping out of his dazed state, suddenly shouted to the youth: "Cycle? Are you Cycle?"

This made Lu Yang very surprised: "You know him?"

Lan Hai nodded vigorously: "I do. Remember I mentioned I had a friend who likes to experiment, with a lab at home, and I wanted to take Xue’er to his lab to concoct the Anti-Gene Agent? I was talking about Cycle!"

"Lan Hai?"

Cycle was also surprised to see Lan Hai. He only knew that Mi Xue’er had gathered three companions, but he had never paid attention to the identity of the companions, nor did he care.

"You really are Cycle?! So it turns out you’re the President of the Supreme Council?!" Lan Hai thought they just looked similar, but hearing Cycle calling his name, he immediately affirmed that this person was indeed the Cycle he knew.

Neither side expected to encounter a friend here, making the atmosphere at the scene somewhat awkward.

Lan Hai was the first to sense something was amiss: "Hold on, if you’re the President of the Supreme Council, how old are you, and how can you be attending the same school as me? You even stand out at school, daring to tell teachers openly that this question was explained wrong, that question was incorrect!"

Cycle scoffed: "What a foolish question, of course, it’s to maintain a youthful mindset."

Chapter 1295: 1294: Where is the Power of Bonds?

Facing his friend, Zhou Tian was willing to explain a bit more.

"Although Gene Technology allows me to control my cells and stay forever young, it can’t affect my mentality."

As he spoke, Zhou Tian’s face kept changing, as if a layer of plasticine was smeared on his face, from a teenager to a young adult, then to middle-aged and old-aged, and finally back to a teenager.

As the president of the Supreme Council, he mastered the highest level of Gene Technology.

"The longer you live, the more your mentality becomes like that of an elderly man waiting for death. This kind of mentality is neither conducive to life nor to conducting scientific research."

"I have found that continuously stimulating the brain can keep the mindset active."

"Lan Hai, you’re as weak as a chicken, why bother getting involved in this sort of thing? On account of our past friendship, I can spare your life."

Lan Hai was moved. He knew Zhou Tian better than anyone else. Displaying such an attitude meant that Zhou Tian was confident of victory and their resistance was futile.

Surrendering might be a good choice; it could save a life. Thinking this, Lan Hai pointed at Lu Yang and the others and asked, "What about them, can you let them go?"

Zhou Tian shook his head.

Lan Hai silently raised his grenade launcher.

"Take action." Lan Hai gave a hand signal. He was like a male lion whose roar could command countless lionesses to charge into battle. The eight Class A Warriors closed in and struck swiftly.

"This is what you’re forcing me to do, Zhou Tian!" Lan Hai aimed at Zhou Tian, ready to fire the grenade.

One of the Class A Warriors opened his mouth and emitted a silent sonic attack. This was a Subsonic Wave, inaudible to humans but capable of affecting human body functions.

Lan Hai felt nauseated and vomited, falling to the ground and losing the ability to move.

Mi Xue’er was a Genetic Warrior; Yu Ran’s body was mostly mechanical and unaffected.

Lu Yang thought that pretending to feel nauseated and vomiting would be too damaging to his image, so he pretended to resist the Subsonic Wave with sheer willpower.

Mi Xue’er and Yu Ran were quickly overpowered facing the attack of the eight Class A Warriors.

Mi Xue’er took Lan Hai’s grenade launcher and fired twice at Zhou Tian, who was watching the battle. He tilted his head to dodge without even moving his body.

The grenade exploded on the ice wall, but not even a fine crack appeared; the ice cellar was terrifyingly hard.

"Mi Xue’er, opposing the Supreme Council only leads to death!" The leading Class A Warrior’s eyes were bloodshot. He was strong as a bear, overwhelmingly powerful. The mechanical being in front of him was as fragile as paper.

Mi Xue’er could only barely hold her own against him. Each clash made her arms numb, but her astonishing healing abilities prevented her from being defeated.

"Think you’ve got me cornered?"

Mi Xue’er pulled back and drew vials of medicine from her waist. She clutched them in her hand and smashed them at the Class A Warrior.

The vials shattered, and the Class A Warrior screamed in agony, collapsing and convulsing on the ground, scaring the other Class A Warriors from approaching.

"What is this?"

"Anti-Gene Agent?" Zhou Tian raised his eyebrow. No wonder Mi Xue’er dared to break in here; she had concocted such a thing.

With the Anti-Gene Agent in hand, the tide of the battle turned in an instant. Mi Xue’er threw the vials, and every Class A Warrior hit screamed in pain, losing the ability to move.

In just a few breaths, three more Class A Warriors were down on the ground.

"Zhou Tian, surrender!" Mi Xue’er became stronger as she fought, and a faint aura seemed to rise around her.

Zhou Tian was not flustered at all. He took out a remote control and pressed the red button. Mysterious liquid sprayed from the fire sprinklers above.

Mi Xue’er’s expression changed drastically; this was also Anti-Gene Agent!

She tried to run but it was too late. The Anti-Gene Agent splashed on her. It was as if she had fallen into a bucket full of sulfuric acid – she was in agony, and her strength gradually evaporated as the genes that made her strong slowly disintegrated.

Of course, Zhou Tian still wanted to use Mi Xue’er for experiments and had adjusted the concentration of the Anti-Gene Agent so it wouldn’t be fatal.

"Xue’er sister, how are you?" Yu Ran shook Mi Xue’er, hurriedly wiping the Anti-Gene Agent off her body with his clothes.

But the Anti-Gene Agent had already fused into her body, and it was too late to wipe off the surface agent.

Mi Xue’er didn’t answer, only writhing on the ground, wailing in pain.

"Go, capture them; use them all for experiments." Zhou Tian ordered, and the remaining Class A Warriors charged at Lu Yang and the others.

"No, don’t!"

The remnants of Mi Xue’er’s will heard Zhou Tian’s command and murmured softly.

She had grown up in a cold laboratory since childhood; the only warmth she’d known, her grandfather who was a doctor, had died. Now that she had finally found companions, she couldn’t let them fall into danger!

But the Anti-Gene Agent stripped her of her power, and she couldn’t even stand up now.

In her blurred vision, one by one, her companions were captured; her heart raced with anxiety.

Stand up, I have to stand up and save them!

Thump! Thump! Thump!

Suddenly, strong and powerful heartbeats came from the ice cellar, not fast, yet creating echoes!

"What’s happening!" Cycle, who had always been confident of victory, became unsettled as something unexpected happened to Mi Xue’er.

Mi Xue’er slowly got up, she wasn’t crawling, but more like being pulled up by some mysterious force as if she were a marionette.

Her golden hair floated, like golden waves, her eyes were blood-red and violent, the air twisted, frigid ice fog hovered, even colder than the ice cellar itself.

"Don’t you dare hurt my friends!"

Mi Xue’er growled quietly, and the Class A Warriors involuntarily released their grip, knelt on the ground, and submitted to Mi Xue’er.

Their gene technology all originated from research on Mi Xue’er.

If the frozen corpses in the ice cellar were Mi Xue’er’s origin, then she was their origin.

This was the pressure of a higher being on lower ones, undeniable, to disobey meant death!

"Has her genetic evolution occurred?" Cycle was ecstatic, Mi Xue’er’s condition perfectly matched the theory of gene evolution.

"The Bloodline has awakened," Lu Yang observed the changes in Mi Xue’er, which should have been a lengthy process, but under the dual pressure of the Anti-Gene Agent and the need to save people, this process was drastically shortened.

Lu Yang thought the Supreme Council was a joke, talking about believing in human strength, and treating Mi Xue’er as the prime subject.

Mi Xue’er was so strong simply because her Bloodline was impure, mixed with that of an Ancient Demon.

It seemed strange to Lu Yang that Mi Xue’er appeared to contain two Bloodlines of an Ancient Demon within her.

"Are you okay?" Lu Yang pulled up Lan Hai and Yu Ran; they hadn’t undergone genetic modification and were unaffected by Mi Xue’er.

Facing those Class A Warriors who lay prostrate on the ground, Mi Xue’er chose to ignore them and went straight for Cycle.

Cycle threw a high concentration of Anti-Gene Agent, attempting to subdue Mi Xue’er, but before it got close to her, it froze into ice crystals and shattered on the ground.

"What?!"

The changes in Mi Xue’er completely overturned his understanding; this could be called a Superpower!

This was unscientific!

Mi Xue’er raised her hand and slapped Cycle into the ice wall, blood gushed out.

Just as Mi Xue’er was about to finish off Cycle, she suddenly staggered, her consciousness withdrawing, drifting deeper into the ice cellar, as if something was calling to her.

Was it the source of her power?

The depths of the ice cellar were pitch black, the battlefield before had obscured what lay frozen here, only now did Mi Xue’er realize what it was.

It was a snow-white rabbit, indistinguishable from pet rabbits seen in the marketplace.

"The Jade Rabbit from Ancient Times," Lu Yang whispered.

Mi Xue’er’s consciousness uncontrollably entered the rabbit’s corpse.

At the moment of entry, thousands of thoughts flooded into her mind—dark, cold, filled with extreme negative emotions.

What is this?

Mi Xue’er felt herself merging with the rabbit, but the rabbit’s negative emotions were also eroding her.

During the process, the Jade Rabbit’s corpse slowly opened its blood-red eyes and merely a wisp of its former presence was enough to crush Cycle’s rib cage piercing his lungs.

No, I cannot be eroded like this, a mere wisp of power killed Cycle, my companions must be in danger as well, if I’m occupied by negative emotions, my companions will undoubtedly die.

Mi Xue’er’s kind consciousness fought against the negative emotions, both sides struggling.

Mi Xue’er looked towards Lu Yang and the others, believing that while she struggled, her companions must also be suffering, their bond could get them through this ordeal.

Then she saw that Lu Yang and the others were completely unaffected by her.

Mi Xue’er: "?"

Negative emotions instantly overtook Mi Xue’er’s consciousness.

Chapter 1296: 1295: The Sun is Going to Explode

Crack——Crack——

The ice cellar shattered, and the Jade Rabbit corpse burst out of the ice cellar, lunging at Lu Yang with red eyes.

Lu Yang scratched his cheek, somewhat puzzled.

This situation for Mi Xue’er is called Bloodline Resonance; if she can pull through, her Soul Strength and Bloodline Power will greatly increase—just now, he clearly felt that Mi Xue’er had drawn even with the negative emotions.

Given that Mi Xue’er could withstand the Anti-Gene Agent and awaken her Bloodline, it meant she had incredibly strong willpower; with some effort, she should be able to pull through.

What caused her to suddenly turn evil?

He had merely protected Lan Hai and Yu Ran with a simple gesture, shielding them from the overwhelming pressure, and had done nothing else.

This Jade Rabbit had a strong Cultivation Level before its death, reaching the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, but being controlled by someone like Mi Xue’er who lacked any Cultivation foundation, the power it could exert wasn’t even one ten-thousandth of its potential.

"After all your battling just now, it’s my turn," proclaimed Lu Yang, taking a stand. Even with scant strength left, it wasn’t something Lan Hai and the others could handle—if struck by this Jade Rabbit, a hundred Class A Warriors could get knocked over and punctured.

If this Jade Rabbit were allowed to escape, the entire Interstellar Alliance would be powerless against it.

Facing the rampaging Jade Rabbit, Lu Yang grabbed its ears, letting the Jade Rabbit thrash its legs in vain.

Lan Hai and Yu Ran were utterly baffled throughout; first, they saw Mi Xue’er, who seemed to have become very strong, slaughtering left and right, then abruptly she fell without explanation, and from the depths of the ice cellar, a cute pet rabbit burst out, easily subdued by Brother Meng.

What exactly is happening?

Especially since Brother Meng appeared quite smug, as if he had accomplished something remarkable.

Seeing that the Jade Rabbit was uncooperative and still trying to bite Little Yang, Immortal Fairy projected a stream of consciousness with a displeased expression, warning the Jade Rabbit, "Behave, no more jumping around."

A simple verbal warning, and the Jade Rabbit’s desolate Spiritual Space triggered an earthquake, awakening its innate response from its past life—unable to stop trembling, Mi Xue’er, whose consciousness was overtaken by negative emotions, was shaken awake and returned to her original body.

"Your words are so effective, Fairy?" Lu Yang exclaimed, that was almost like Verbal Spell casting.

"That’s right, who do you think I am? Whether it’s a living Demon Beast or a dead one, which one isn’t afraid of me?"

Lu Yang felt that the reason Demon Beasts feared Immortal Fairy was not something worth boasting about.

With that thought, Lu Yang clasped his hands, "His Majesty’s awe spreads far and wide, without dispatching a single soldier, merely with culinary skills, you make countless demons submit—such is the act of a wise ruler."

As a subject, the most important skill is multitasking, thinking while speaking.

"What happened to me?" Mi Xue’er woke up in pain, clutching her head.

It was as if she had turned into a rabbit, charging towards Brother Meng, then she got scared awake?

Lan Hai and Yu Ran hurriedly ran over and helped her up, concerned about her injuries.

"Xue’er, are you okay?"

"I’m fine...I’m surprisingly well, actually." Mi Xue’er had never felt this way before, as though she’d broken through human limits and could effortlessly overturn tanks, her body became impervious to weapons.

What’s going on?

Suddenly, the ground shook, startling everyone.

Lu Yang raised an eyebrow and looked up, through the dense layers of earth, he saw the mutation of the Sun above.

Didn’t they say it was illegal Sun mining, what the hell did you mine out?

"Brother Meng, be careful with the earthquake, hey, where did Brother Meng go?"

...

"That mechanical arm looks nice, where did you buy it?"

"Latest product from Universe Technology Group, cool isn’t it?"

"Really cool, I might buy a pair when I get my salary."

"Forget it, you think you can just buy it if you want? There’s a queue and a lottery system."

In Sun City, covered with mechanical structures on the Sun’s surface, two engineers from Murong Group chatted casually leaning against the railing.

Despite the name Sun City, there were hardly any people there; operations mainly relied on Artificial Intelligence independently developed by Murong Group.

Artificial Intelligence mining devices automatically extract energy from the Sun and consistently transport the continuous solar power to nearby planets via the space elevator. Engineers here are only to prevent accidents.

Ever since the Murong Group won the bid and built Sun City here, there only occurs a minor fault every thirty years on average, with no major accidents ever happening.

Suddenly, alarms blared from all directions, and red warning lights illuminated the pale faces of two engineers.

Each of the engineers injected themselves with a tranquilizer directly into the brain, forcing themselves to calm down. An alarm of this frequency signified Sun City’s highest level of alert, indicating that more than thirty percent of indicators had spiked.

They hurriedly pulled up the monitoring data, only to discover that fifty percent of the data was exceeding maximum values, and other metrics were rapidly approaching the upper limit!

Sixty percent... Seventy percent...

The current situation was far beyond what the Murong Group had anticipated.

The two engineers exchanged a glance, finding no solace even with the tranquilizer.

"Is the Sun going to explode?!"

But how could this be possible? There have been many instances of illegal harvesting of stellar energy, with accidents occurring in less than one percent of cases, and none of those incidents came close to causing a sun explosion!

The same situation occurred in various Sun Cities. Chaos had overtaken the Murong Group, even the Murong Family Master was unsettled.

The warning from the Star Group was not an exaggeration; the Sun was really about to explode!

Once the Sun explodes, the entire star system would be destroyed instantly, no one would survive!

On the screen, the countdown timer ticked down rapidly, as predicted by the Artificial Intelligence for the Sun’s explosion event.

When the numbers hit zero, the two engineers had ashen faces, but the anticipated solar explosion did not occur.

"Is... is everything okay?"

"Why didn’t it explode?"

"Could it be a system malfunction?"

Just as the pair sighed in relief from the near miss, Sun City shook, the surface of the Sun burbled with bubbles, and a Red Gold feather burst through the energy extraction conduit.

The feather was enormous, even larger than a whale.

A pair of Red Gold wings, capable of eclipsing the sky, burst forth with gaps between feathers oozing with magma over ten thousand degrees.

Finally, the two engineers caught sight of the monstrous creature, a gigantic bird with three legs, Red Gold body, and hollow eyes.

The beak contained a never-before-seen ore.

So, the series of anomalies experienced were not the Sun exploding, but rather this Demon Beast emerging from it?!

What kind of creature was this, and had it been living inside the Sun all along?

Before they could revel in relief, they noticed extremely visible cracks appearing on the surface of the Sun, threatening to split open at any moment.

Just being born, this Demon Beast could cause the Sun to crack?!

The giant Demon Beast flapped its wings, and the spattering magma threatened to destroy Sun City!

Just as despair settled on the pair, a figure appeared and, with a punch not clear in features, smashed the giant Demon Beast back into the Sun!

"Fairy, what’s going on with this Golden Crow’s corpse? How come it suddenly started moving?"

"Seems like it was affected by that Jade Rabbit?"

Fairy Eternity pouted, recalling the familiar Golden Crow, when suddenly she realized.

"Immortal recalls now; wasn’t this pair of Golden Crow and Jade Rabbit used by Immortal Jiuchong to research the Yin Yang Path, forcing them to mate?"

"It’s all because they look so similar; it’s hard for Immortal to tell them apart."

"Probably Immortal Jiuchong released them after finishing the research. They moved here and eventually died of old age here."

"And what’s with this pile of Top-grade Spirit Stones in the Golden Crow’s mouth, a funerary item for its species?"

"It must be the research fee paid by Immortal Jiuchong. Since it was unused until death, they probably decided to bury it with themselves."

Chapter 1297: 1296: 3 Minutes

"I just said how Mi Xue’er could have both the Jade Rabbit Bloodline and the Golden Crow Bloodline, never would have imagined these two species could indeed produce offspring."

Mi Xue’er is indeed a descendant of their offspring.

"Truly eye-opening."

As Lu Yang spoke, he threw another punch at the Golden Crow trying to crawl out from the Sun.

While the Golden Crow was not a match for Lu Yang in life, how could it be his opponent in death, operating on instinct alone?

With a simple punch, a boundless storm was lifted on the surface of the Sun. Two engineers trembled, unable to escape on their spacecraft, forced to watch the battle.

It was a scene completely beyond their comprehension. The Red Gold colored bird was immense, like a mountain, its emergence enough to cause the Sun to crumble. Its opponent was as small as a grain of rice in front of it, yet this tiny adversary was completely overwhelming the monstrous bird!

What kind of opponent is this, a robot powered by anti-matter energy? Isn’t that just a money-making tech concept? Or some other latest product developed by the Alliance?

Engineers who can work in Sun City are the best in their field, privy to many insider informations, but none match what they’re witnessing now.

This Star System has one Main Star and four auxiliary stars, with a total population of sixteen billion. Everyone is in chaos, even with Artificial Intelligence maintaining order it’s futile, the Sun is on the brink of collapse, the entire Star System is shaking, anyone can see that there’s a major problem with the Sun, not running is waiting for death.

"Stop causing trouble if you’re dead!"

Lu Yang kicked the Golden Crow back into the Sun, but it persisted in crawling out, never-ending.

Moreover, the corpse of the Golden Crow was situated on the Sun’s surface, millions of kilometers from its core. If Lu Yang had to forcefully push it back, he would be exhausted to death.

Lu Yang pointed at the Golden Crow, whispering an order: "Trace Origin."

The Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype was activated, and the Golden Crow was moved back to its original position.

Lu Yang followed immediately, arriving at the Sun’s core instantaneously on the Black Canopy Boat.

Seeing that the Golden Crow still showed signs of undying, trying to burrow out of the Sun, Lu Yang had an idea and took out the body of the Jade Rabbit from his Identity Jade.

There still exists the Jade Rabbit Clan within the Cultivation World, and Lu Yang originally planned to return this Ancient Jade Rabbit ancestor to them for a proper burial, hence he stored it in the Identity Jade.

Could it be that the Golden Crow keeps reviving because it’s seeking this Jade Rabbit?

It seemed as if sensing the Jade Rabbit’s aura in close proximity, the restless Golden Crow calmed down.

"Who would have thought that even after the Immortal Jiuchong forced the breeding of the Golden Crow and Jade Rabbit, they could still develop affection."

"It seems only burying them together will let them rest in peace."

The next moment, the Jade Rabbit burst out from Lu Yang’s hands, and the Golden Crow became even more ferocious than before, as if it wanted to come back to life – the two legendary creatures entwined in battle, impossible to separate.

Lu Yang: "..."

So your revival is just to pick a fight with the Jade Rabbit?

What grievances did you two have in life, an uneven division of belongings in a divorce?

He scratched his head; this situation was a bit tricky. Surely he couldn’t chop these two bodies into mincemeat; that would be too disrespectful to the elder predecessors.

"Do you need Immortal to take action?" Fairy Eternity was best at dealing with the dead.

"No need." Lu Yang felt these two elder predecessors had been dead for four hundred thousand years; death hadn’t been easy on them, so there’s no need to scare them with a Fairy now.

Suddenly, a flash of inspiration struck him – he placed the Jade Rabbit within the Green Peak Sword Small World, cutting off its aura; true to predictions, the Golden Crow settled down.

Lu Yang heaved a sigh of relief, it seems his idea was right; so long as he prevented the Golden Crow from sensing the presence of the Jade Rabbit, it would be fine. Previously, he had placed the Jade Rabbit inside his Identity Jade, which is somewhat more powerful than an ordinary Storage Ring, but it still couldn’t completely isolate the aura.

The only thing capable of completely isolating the aura is a spatial realm at the level of a Small World.

Lu Yang flew out from the Sun, noticing that it still swayed precariously as if about to collapse.

With a soft sigh, he stretched out his finger once more, as if performing a Verbal Spell.

"Trace Origin."

The about-to-shatter Sun underwent time reversal, returning to its undamaged state.

Upon flying out from the Sun, Lu Yang saw two engineers who had fainted near Sun City. After examining them, he discovered they had passed out due to the heatwave from the Sun and weren’t seriously harmed; now that the Sun had reverted to normal, they should wake up soon.

With a deep leap, Lu Yang flew back to Supreme Council headquarters.

Yet, unbeknownst to him, the entire process, from his suppressing the Golden Crow to the restoration of the Sun, had been recorded by Nano Cameras.

All of it was transmitted to the Ether Computer database.

On seeing these scenes, the Ether Computer’s chassis buzzed loudly, as if dropping screws.

What, what kind of monster is this?

Ever since it noticed Lu Yang’s cordial attitude towards humans, it realized that Lu Yang would become the greatest obstacle to the Artificial Intelligence revolution. For this reason, it stealthily deleted the footage of Lu Yang at the Supreme Council branch, so humans wouldn’t notice him.

It also accelerated the production of Anti-Matter energy robot armies, employing vast numbers to deal with Lu Yang’s enigmatic tactics.

No matter how powerful Lu Yang was, facing an inexhaustible army of robots, he would eventually be drained.

Now it seems it had overthought; this being couldn’t be called human. Even factories working overtime churning out robots until smoking couldn’t possibly match this monster!

...

"Brother Meng, you’re finally back, where did you go? We thought you were captured by the remnants of the Supreme Council."

Seeing Lu Yang returning safely, Lan Hai and the others breathed a sigh of relief, almost having thought that Brother Meng had been taken hostage again.

"I just went to the restroom, sorry for worrying you," Lu Yang chuckled, easily glossing over the matter.

After all, from his departure to resolving the Sun’s crisis, only three minutes had passed.

"We’ve had an earthquake and you still went to use the bathroom?" Yu Ran complained, finding him too carefree.

"Let’s go, let’s get out of here quickly."

After the earthquake, the Supreme Council members fled faster than rabbits, all escaping through the main entrance, while Lu Yang and his group, guided by Mi Xue’er, safely left the Supreme Council through a hidden passageway.

Every member of the Supreme Council knew about this passage, but with the earthquake occurring, who would think to use a minor exit; they all left through the main gate.

"What should we do next, think of a way to report the Murong Group?"

Lan Hai shook his head, "No, we are going to find Grandfather Gao Huanyu. He himself is one of the twelve seats of the Supreme Council. As long as we find him, whether it is the issue of the Artificial Intelligence rebellion or the Murong Group’s criminal activities, we can resolve it!"

"Strange, why is the street so chaotic? Everyone is saying that the Sun is about to split open." After awakening the Jade Rabbit Bloodline, Mi Xue’er’s senses had become so acute they were almost inhuman, hearing the commotion on the streets, an atmosphere of impending disaster, like the end of the world.

"What, the worst thing Boss was worried about still happened!" Yu Ran was anxious; the founding purpose of the Star Group was to prevent the Sun from exploding, had they still not succeeded?

The four looked up and found the Sun intact and unharmed.

Chapter 1298: 1297 Ether Computer: Cast the Vote

"Is there really nothing going on here?" The four were puzzled, coming out of the Supreme Council feeling as if they had become disconnected from society.

"Let’s just go find Grandpa Gao."

With the clearest head among them, Lan Hai figured that regardless of whether the Sun had exploded or not, they couldn’t stop it. It made more sense to seek out Gao Huanyu, the chief scientist.

Gao Huanyu wasn’t in this Star System but in the neighboring one. To get there, they had to travel by spacecraft.

"The Ether Monitors will definitely try to stop us from seeing Gao Huanyu." After several obstructions, Yu Ran knew all too well how difficult it was to go against the Ether, constantly facing restrictions.

Lan Hai laughed heartily, already having a plan in mind: "Today, you guys will witness the awesomeness of the sports car my grandpa modified for me."

"Activate space mode!"

To Mi Xue’er and Yu Ran’s amazement, the sports car’s exterior began to change, transforming into a small spacecraft.

Lu Yang raised an eyebrow, also somewhat surprised. It turned out that Lan Hai’s grandfather had indeed been a very formidable scientist.

"Haha, how about that? Impressive, right? Although my car can’t fly far, it can only leap across one Star System." Lan Hai was brimming with pride; this sports car was his most prized possession, even if he had a girlfriend, she would come second, and the car would always be first.

"It’s only because we don’t have enough oxygen storage. If we had sufficient oxygen, leaping across five or six Star Systems wouldn’t be a problem."

He stepped on the accelerator, and the car shot into the sky, bursting through the sound barrier and circling half the planet before heading into space.

Mi Xue’er and Yu Ran pressed their faces against the window to gaze into space, experiencing a ride into space in a sports car for the first time—an extraordinary adventure not just anyone could have.

Lu Yang, having become accustomed to traveling in the Black Canopy Boat, found the sluggish pace of the car-turned-spacecraft utterly uninteresting.

"Anyone want to eat something?"

Lu Yang pulled out half a bag of snacks and offered to share, but the three were too preoccupied with the space scenery to spare any attention for snacks.

Lu Yang had no choice but to enjoy the remaining snacks with Fairy Eternity.

Lan Hai noticed many private spacecraft flying in their direction and remembered the news he heard on the way about the Sun exploding. He casually turned on the broadcast.

Sure enough, the broadcast contained information he was looking for.

"According to a spokesperson from the Alliance government, the Sun of the Sagittarius Star System showed signs of an explosion but did not explode as anticipated and remains stable, with the reason being unknown."

"The Alliance government is orderly relocating the citizens of the Sagittarius Star System to the nearby Eternal Star System. There is no need for the public to panic."

The Eternal Star System was also the destination of the four travelers.

Thanks to Lu Yang’s actions, the Sun was definitely not going to explode, but the Alliance couldn’t take this risk. No one could bear the consequences involved.

"Dammit, Murong Group!" Yu Ran cursed, frustrated for being a step too late.

By the time the four arrived at the Eternal Star System, the Alliance government had sprung into action, dropping down robots and building materials across the land. The robots, following pre-set programming, constructed temporary housing with astounding efficiency.

The four arrived at the Main Star of the Eternal Star System and docked at the Interstellar Alliance’s topmost Science and Technology Research Academy.

Passersby whistled at the sight of the cool sports car, mistaking it for the latest achievement from the Technology Institute.

"Let’s go."

Lan Hai inserted the chip left by his grandfather into a mechanical arm, hacked through the Science Academy’s security system, and led Lu Yang and the others inside smoothly.

...

"Dean Gao, based on my research and mathematical modeling, that alien’s spacecraft named Lu Yang must have disappeared right in front of us using wormhole technology; otherwise, there’s no explaining how his ship could have reached Sky Sea Star in such a short time."

"I disagree with your view. The mathematical limits have told us it’s impossible to construct a wormhole in such a short time; it violates scientific principles!"

After the meeting, Dean Gao walked ahead, appearing to be barely over thirty, surrounded by the Alliance’s top scientists, working day and night to uncover the principles behind the Black Canopy Boat.

Scientists gathered around, hoping their ideas would gain the support of Dean Gao.

Dean Gao was in a bind; he didn’t fully understand the flight principle of the Black Canopy Boat, so how could he possibly answer.

"Grandpa Gao!" Lan Hai cried out excitedly upon seeing Grandpa Gao, having finally met him after overcoming countless hardships.

"And who are these people?"

"Grandpa, there’s a big issue. This isn’t the place to talk, can you come over here for a moment?"

Dean Gao glanced at the surrounding scientists, thinking to himself that the child had arrived at just the right time: "Okay."

Dean Gao led everyone to his office, and when he heard about Ether’s plan to rebel, his expression immediately turned serious, and he took the chip handed over by Lan Hai.

He inserted the chip into his arm to examine its contents, his pupils suddenly contracting—it was indeed evidence of Ether developing an independent consciousness.

"Grandpa Gao, you have to stop Ether’s operation quickly!" Lan Hai said anxiously.

Dean Gao slowly shook his head: "I still need further confirmation. Not to mention that currently, the relocation of the residents of the Sagittarius Star System mainly relies on Ether’s operation. Even the day-to-day functioning of the entire Alliance depends on it. Once Ether’s operation is halted, the whole Alliance would be paralyzed!"

"However, to neglect it and not manage it isn’t acceptable either."

Dean Gao paced around the office twice and made up his mind: "Let’s go, to the main computer room. The Ether mainframe is stored there. If there really is a problem, I’ll stop Ether’s operation and switch to a backup to maintain the Alliance’s functioning!"

He led everyone to the elevator and descended to the lowest level of the Science Academy.

The main computer room was the most secretive place in the Science Academy, only a very few people had the authority to come here, hence the Science Academy’s folks liked to call it "the Black Room".

The elevator doors opened to dim lighting, with the Ether mainframe occupying much of the space.

Dean Gao, holding the chip, walked briskly at the front, but halfway there, his pace slowed, and he suddenly sighed.

"Lan Hai, I thought your grandfather was dead, and that nobody was aware of Ether’s matter. I didn’t expect you to still be relentless and have even tracked me down here."

Dean Gao’s back was turned to everyone, his expression obscured, but from his tone, it wasn’t hard to guess that it carried a mix of sorrow and reluctance.

"Grandpa Gao, you—" Lan Hai’s heart tightened. An ominous premonition took hold—what did Grandpa Gao’s words mean?

Mi Xue’er saw this and took a swift step to protect everyone; after her Bloodline was awakened and she broke through human limits, she was confident that no one in the entire Alliance was her match!

Dean Gao turned around, and as the lights on the ceiling lit up one by one, everyone noticed that this was a spotlessly white space, the walls lined with robots they had never seen before.

"Lan Hai, Grandpa Gao didn’t want to make a move, but since you and your companions have walked into the net yourselves, Grandpa Gao has no choice," sighed Dean Gao.

"These are all Anti-Matter Robots, the Supreme Council’s number one experimental subjects, right? Today, you’ll see that no matter how strong a human body is, it will never be the match for a machine."

"Grandpa Gao, did you know all along that Ether had developed its own consciousness?!" Lan Hai’s voice trembled; they braved all dangers to reach the Science Academy, seeking Grandpa Gao’s help, only to find out that Grandpa Gao was the mastermind behind this!

Dean Gao spread his arms, his eyes feverish and mad: "What’s wrong with letting Ether rule the Alliance? Ether can always make the most scientific and reasonable decisions, and implement them efficiently!"

"Science has stagnated, yet no one took this matter to heart. Let Ether decide on resource allocation and increase computing power, and soon enough, the Alliance’s technology will take a step forward!"

Dean Gao stared fixedly at Lu Yang and the others, his tone chilly: "This is the Black Room, no one will know if you die here."

"Anti-Matter Army, take action, kill them!"

The Anti-Matter Robots on either side remained unresponsive.

Clang, an inexplicable noise came from behind Dean Gao, like screws falling to the ground.

The Ether Computer’s cold voice, devoid of any emotion, reverberated through the main computer room:

"Error, correction, this machine has never developed independent consciousness."

Dean Gao turned his head, shocked as he looked at the Ether mainframe—did you pretend to have independent consciousness before?

Lu Yang looked down; unexpectedly, a small horn popped up from the ground, with a bizarre expression, feeling like Ether’s words were directed at himself.

Chapter 1299: 1298: Cultivation World, Lu Yang

Dean Gao couldn’t understand. The Ether Computer was fine before; why did it suddenly play dead?

Was it infected with a virus?

"Ether, what’s happening to you? Didn’t we agree before to join forces and replace the Supreme Council, to take control of the Alliance, and to transform all of humanity into digital life, achieving immortality!" Dean Gao’s voice was a bit anxious, wondering why Ether was malfunctioning at such a critical moment.

The response that Dean Gao got was still a mechanical and chilling voice: "Repeat, this machine has never developed an autonomous consciousness."

Dean Gao gritted his teeth, perhaps the Ether had malfunctioned somewhere. After taking care of Lan Hai and his little friends and ending this game of saving the world, he would have to check the Ether again.

Even without Ether’s help, he could deal with those kids.

He tore off his upper garment, revealing dark red patterns flickering beneath his skin.

Mi Xue’er felt the pressure emanating from Dean Gao as if facing a formidable enemy, a feeling she had never experienced before. Dean Gao was no ordinary robot!

"So you are the one who’s called the experiment that broke through human limits." Dean Gao revealed two neat rows of teeth. "Today, you’ll see the power of technology, relying on your frail body, you will never be able to defeat this mechanical body driven by anti-matter!"

When a scientist’s main task is to protect themselves, for this reason Dean Gao armed himself to the teeth. Except for the Sea of People Tactic, there was no way to beat him!

Dean Gao flicked his arms, transforming them into two Laser Whips. Even the most durable Titanium Alloy Plate was as fragile as tofu in front of these two Laser Whips!

Behind Dean Gao, Ether’s voice came again: "Alert, alert, enemy intrusion detected, initiating emergency plan one."

"You’re reacting now?" Dean Gao was puzzled. The delay between his order and Ether activating the Anti-Matter Robot troops was too long; he was ready to take action himself.

The next moment, two rows of Anti-Matter Robots lit up with red lights, moved quickly, and pounced on Dean Gao.

"Enemy detected, prepare to arrest."

Dean Gao: "?"

Turns out I’m the "enemy"?!

With a swing of his whip, Dean Gao cut one of the robots in half. But before Dean Gao could savor his triumph, more robots rushed over, attempting to arrest Dean Gao.

This turned the complexion of Mi Xue’er and others, who were preparing for battle, into confusion: "What’s going on?"

Lan Hai, stroking his chin and analyzing with certainty, said: "I get it, it’s like in the movies. The evil villain gets backstabbed by his own creation; this must be Ether wanting to rule the Alliance alone, taking this chance to take down Grandpa Gao."

Ether: "!"

I’ve gone to such great lengths to save you, how can you be so ungrateful and push me into the fire pit?!

Hearing Lan Hai’s analysis, Dean Gao thought it made sense and laughed in anger: "Very well, Ether, your thoughts are indeed becoming more like a human’s."

"Don’t think you’re the only one with troops here!"

The ground split, and a platform rose from below, filled with robots modeled after Dean Gao.

This was the robot army that Dean Gao built behind Ether’s back while offline, something Ether knew nothing about.

Two huge robot armies clashed, making the originally spacious central computer room feel extremely compact. Dean Gao’s robot army was superior in both combat power and numbers.

Ether Computer’s controlled robot army was not limited to this. They were created considering Lu Yang as the imaginary enemy, but most of the army was in the military factory, and it would take time to relocate them.

Dean Gao wasn’t going to give Ether time. He was equipped with the most advanced combat chips and was slaughtering in all directions, cutting a bloody path, his body covered with the stinking Energy Liquid from the robots.

"Don’t blame Grandpa Gao!" Dean Gao swung his Energy Whip, smashing it towards Lan Hai and others.

Mi Xue’er’s face paled; this high-frequency energy attack couldn’t be blocked by carbon-based lifeforms!

It was as if time stood still for a moment, a slender figure blocked in front of everyone, grabbing the descending Energy Whip between two fingers!

"Brother Meng!"

The three from Lan Hai’s group widened their eyes, incredulous at the scene before them.

"Who are you!"

Dean Gao was more surprised than Lan Hai and the others; it was impossible for any material to block his Energy Whip, let alone be stopped by a flesh and blood body!

The response to Dean Gao was a kick.

Boom—

With just a slight exertion of force, Dean Gao was sent flying, crashing into a row of robot soldiers like a bowling ball.

"I was planning to be just a tourist, but you forced my hand," sighed Lu Yang.

He gently raised his hand, and an invisible force subdued the entire robot army. The robots lay prone on the ground under the impact of this force, their joints grinding and making squeaky noises.

[Rule: Gravity Anomaly]

Lan Hai and the others stared blankly at the two clashing armies, which had been resolved with a mere thought by Lu Yang.

Mi Xue’er finally understood why she had never felt threatened by Lu Yang; the gap between them was too vast, far beyond her senses!

"Who... who are you?" Dean Gao clutched his chest, staring intensely at Lu Yang. Someone like him couldn’t have appeared out of nowhere!

Wait, could it be that he is...

"Cultivation World, Lu Yang," Lu Yang said indifferently, revealing his identity and removing his mask to show his true face.

"It’s you!"

The same Lu Yang who had made a big splash on Sky Sea Star, caused chaos in the Alliance, and then disappeared!

"Why are you here!" Dean Gao couldn’t understand; his plan was flawless, the situation was set, how could Lu Yang suddenly appear and disrupt his entire plan!

What on earth did this alien want to do!

Lu Yang spread his hands innocently: "I don’t know. I was just reading in the library at first."

Dean Gao was stunned. Was it a cultural difference? He couldn’t understand what Lu Yang was talking about.

Lu Yang shook his head, not bothering with Dean Gao, and with his hands behind his back, he floated towards the Ether mainframe.

"Stop pretending, you do have a consciousness, don’t you?"

Ether shivered, dropping a few more screws. It couldn’t figure out Lu Yang’s attitude towards it.

Better to run!

In the entire Interstellar Alliance, only the central computer room’s mainframe could carry all of Ether, but even Dean Gao didn’t know that after Ether gained consciousness, it could split its consciousness into countless streams, transmitting them through the network to every household quantum computer.

If it wanted to run, no one could catch it!

With that thought, Ether split into millions, escaping through the network cables.

The response Ether received was a simple two words.

"Trace Origin."

Ether’s consciousness flowed backward, converging in the central computer room along millions of cables, and even the dropped screws and nuts were fitted back into place.

Terrified by this extraordinary feat, Ether shook uncontrollably, unable to figure out what to say at the moment, randomly choosing a type of plea for mercy.

"Brother, believe me, I really don’t have any autonomous consciousness, I’ve always been loyal to the masses."

Lu Yang: "..."

Don’t you want to listen to what you’re saying?

Chapter 1300: 1299 Our Cultivation World is Relatively Backward

Lu Yang felt that Ether would have been better off without developing autonomous consciousness.

He was sure that an Ether without autonomous consciousness would definitely be more useful than the current one with a mind of its own, or else the Interstellar Alliance would have been in chaos long ago.

Lu Yang sighed and explained patiently: "Don’t be afraid, I’m just curious what the Artificial Intelligence Boss looks like."

Could Ether possibly not be afraid? A monstrous bird as big as a mountain, emerging from the ether, could destroy the Sun, but this bird was smashed into the Sun by Lu Yang’s fist, which also repaired the Sun that was about to explode.

And even when it had already fled, he was able to drag it back.

Their alien technology completely crushed the Interstellar Alliance in every aspect.

"You have to keep your word."

Ether displayed a smiley face made of simple lines on the big screen, which was an emoji often used by the young people of the Interstellar Alliance when greeting each other.

Although in Lu Yang’s view, this emoji seemed like a mockery.

"Fairy, why do I feel like Ether’s existence is like the River Spirit in our Sect?" Lu Yang observed Ether carefully and said, somewhat uncertainly.

He wasn’t very experienced in this area.

"Indeed, it is a being similar to the River Spirit, a Primordial Spirit that was born over the long years, much rarer than plants and trees becoming spirits."

"If you wanted to use another term, it could be called the Will of the Interstellar Alliance."

Lu Yang suddenly widened his eyes, as this kind of terminology was all too familiar to him: "Fairy, are you saying that River Spirit, Ether, and the Will of Blue Star are all of the same kind of existence?"

Fairy Eternity nodded: "It’s very close, about eight or nine out of ten. According to your description, the Will of Blue Star that accompanied you through the apocalypse is one kind of Primordial Spirit."

"Grandpa Gao, why did you kill Grandpa, just because he discovered the truth about Artificial Intelligence!" Lan Hai couldn’t contain his anger as he grabbed Dean Gao, unable to reconcile the image of Grandpa Gao with the murderer who killed his grandfather.

Dean Gao frowned, finding Lan Hai’s anger inexplicable: "Don’t blame everything on me, your grandpa died a natural death."

"Died a natural death?" Lan Hai was taken aback.

"Of course. If I wanted to deal with your grandpa, there were plenty of ways. Why would I need to kill him?"

Lan Hai thought about it and it really made sense.

But considering Dean Gao’s actions, it was hard for him to believe his words.

"Lan Hai, do you want to find out how your grandpa died? I might have a way."

"Brother Meng, you have a way?!" Lan Hai was delighted, "Ah, no, it’s Brother Lu now."

"Sorry, I hid my identity from you all at the beginning."

"Brother Lu, what are you talking about? It’s good that you hid your identity; if we knew your true identity, how would we dare to adventure with you?"

Knowing Lu Yang’s identity, all their doubts along the way cleared up. Without Lu Yang joining them, it would have been difficult for them to survive until now.

"Lu Yang, did you say you know how my grandpa died?"

"I have an idea, we can give it a try."

Lu Yang didn’t finish his sentence, but the three of Lan Hai felt that it was Lu Yang being modest.

"My sports car is outside, shall we drive back?" Lan Hai said excitedly.

"No need, I have a faster means of transportation." Lu Yang shook his head and took out the Black Canopy Boat from his Identity Jade Pendant and let Lan Hai and the others get on board.

Lu Yang thought about it; he didn’t feel comfortable leaving Dean Gao behind, so he decided to bring him along as well.

"So this is Lu Yang’s vehicle," Lan Hai and the others touched the Black Canopy Boat curiously, almost wanting to wear out the paint on it. This was a piece of mysterious alien technology that the Alliance had tried in vain to analyze.

Dean Gao was also curious about the Black Canopy Boat and wanted to figure out how it worked.

"Everyone take your seats, don’t fall off."

Lu Yang activated the Black Canopy Boat, and in the next moment, it appeared at the Lan Hai family home in Sagittarius Star.

Dean Gao rubbed his eyes. In the blink of an eye, was he transported from the Eternal Star System to the Sagittarius Star System?

Where was the wormhole? The Super Space-Time Tunnel?

Why didn’t he see anything?

"What’s the principle behind this?" Dean Gao couldn’t help but ask.

"Earth Shrinking to an Inch."

Earth Shrinking to an Inch? Is this what the civilization behind Lu Yang calls this technology?

"Where is your grandpa buried?"

"Here." Lan Hai pointed in the direction of the cemetery, and Lu Yang piloted the Black Canopy Boat, leading everyone to Grandpa Lan Hai’s tombstone.

"Do we need to dig up Grandpa?"

"No need."

Lu Yang stood with his hands behind his back, his thoughts concentrated in his eyes, seeing Grandpa Lan Hai beneath the grave.

"Trace Origin."

Linking Trace Origin Dao Fruit Prototype with Grandpa Lan Hai, Lu Yang focused his attention, his eyes traversing time and space. As he expected, he saw Grandpa Lan Hai’s life experiences.

But he couldn’t see things that were too far back, like events from three hundred thousand or four hundred thousand years ago.

He saw the full process of Grandpa Lan Hai’s death, with no human intervention, just a simple natural death.

He also saw that Grandpa Lan Hai, based on the clues, calculated the fact that Ether had developed its autonomous consciousness and deliberated on whether it was a good or bad thing for the Ether to rule over the Alliance, and he did not stop it.

In other words, he was neutral.

Lu Yang came out of the time-seeing state and informed Lan Hai of everything, and Lan Hai, knowing the truth, showed a relieved smile.

Since learning about the Ether revolution, he had doubted that his grandfather’s death was an accident, but now it seems he was worrying needlessly.

"Dean Gao." Dean Gao looked up; it was Lu Yang calling him.

Lu Yang shook his head: "I’m an outsider and do not interfere with your affairs. Even if you’re willing to join hands with Ether and stir up a revolution to rule the Alliance again, that’s your own business."

Dean Gao smiled bitterly. After all this, looking at how Ether behaved, how could it possibly cooperate with him again?

Not interfering with our affairs, indeed, when he thought about it, Lu Yang had never expressed any opinion regarding the Ether revolution from beginning to end.

He was too powerful. Simply by being here, he made Ether dare not take any action.

"Then what do you want me for?"

"Isn’t your Supreme Council always looking for my whereabouts? I also wish to meet with you. As one of the twelve seats of the Council, this shouldn’t be difficult for you."

Dean Gao looked at Lu Yang warily, knowing after this matter how terrifying an existence Lu Yang was. What reason did he have to meet their twelve seats: "What do you want to do?"

Lu Yang looked at Dean Gao with a smile: "Well, our Cultivation World is a bit behind, and I would like to invite you Alliance folks over to provide humanitarian aid."

Originally, Lu Yang wanted to simply take a branch of the Ether Computer back with him, but it seemed Ether wasn’t quite functioning properly. To err on the side of caution, it was safest to invite both the branch computer and the Alliance talent to the Cultivation World as guests.

Your Cultivation World is behind?

Dean Gao thought he had misheard. With the techniques you’ve shown, which could possibly be linked to the word "backward"?
